Area 1 holds a Chozo settlement close to the surface, temple-like halls among water and plants. Area 2's flooded caverns contain Chozo ruins and heavy machinery; atop its largest installation a Chozo Statue cradles not an item but Arachnus, curled in an orb. Area 3 is a sprawling mining complex of Chozo robotics and digging equipment, where the Diggernaut lay buried. Area 7 is the Chozo Laboratory proper: dark vertical shafts, chambers of vats filled with green ooze beneath Metroid imagery, and a great planetarium dome, where three Omega Metroids laired. The vats and imagery suggest that this is where the Thoha made the Metroids that hunted the X Parasites.

Automated defenses still guard these places: Autracks, neck-extending turrets that fire laser shots; Wallfires, wall-mounted sentries that spit fireballs; hopping Autoads; and Gunzoos patrolling the shafts (see Fauna of SR388). Chozo Statues hold components of the Power Suit, and Samus took most of her arsenal from them, from the Bombs, Ice Beam and Spider Ball of Area 1 to the Plasma Beam, Gravity Suit and Screw Attack of Area 5; the Spring Ball she won from Arachnus, and the Space Jump and Power Bomb she had to wrest from the Diggernaut. Aeion Ability Artifacts conferred the Scan Pulse, Lightning Armor, Beam Burst and Phase Drift, all powered by Aeion, which SR388 emits and which the Chozo first extracted from the planet's creatures.

The Chozo Seals are the colony's locks. Each is a golden disc bearing a Metroid insignia, ringed with orbs that light to count the Metroids remaining in its area. A seal accepts Metroid DNA and drains the corrosive purple liquid flooding the way downward, so the colony's depths open only as its Metroids die. The Chozo Memories, the colony's pictorial record of its founding, its Aeion extraction, the X, the Metroids and a final confrontation between two Chozo leaders, are scattered through these ruins.

Samus was raised by the Chozo of Zebes and armed herself on SR388 from the statues of their kin. Only on ZDR, from Quiet Robe, did she learn that this colony had been Thoha and that Raven Beak had slaughtered it.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Aeion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Autrack · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Wallfire · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Arachnus · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Raven_Beak · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe</sub>


### SR388: The Federation's lost expeditions to SR388 and the extermination order

*Keys:* special squadron, elite squadron, extermination order, order to exterminate, special combat group, search and rescue party, rescue party, research ship, lost expeditions  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* SR388, SR-388, SR 388, Metroid, Metroids, extermination, exterminate


After Samus Aran destroyed Mother Brain on Zebes, the Galactic Federation weighed how terrible and destructive the Metroid life form was, a creature that multiplied on mere exposure to beta rays, and sent another research ship to SR388 to make sure no Metroids were left on the planet. Per the original manual, an emergency notice soon came from the research base: contact had been lost and the research ship was missing. The base had already sent a search and rescue party, but after its initial contact the rescue ship was never heard from again. A special combat group of armed soldiers from the Federation Police was dispatched at once; it transmitted its primary landing data and then fell silent as well. Rumors spread fast, and the galaxy was again seized with fear of Metroids. Convinced that at least one Metroid survived deep underground, and that even one living Metroid could easily wipe out an entire planetary civilization, the Federation called its members to an urgent conference, which unanimously agreed to give Samus Aran the order to exterminate the Metroids.

The later account (Metroid: Samus Returns) condenses this: the Federation sent a special squadron of elite soldiers to investigate SR388, the squadron went missing, and after retrieving a small sampling of data confirming a Metroid presence within the planet, the Federation commissioned Samus to wipe out the species. A dead Federation soldier lay near a door by her landing site. The Space Pirates' interest in SR388 never lapsed: Proteus Ridley came to the planet to take the hatchling from Samus as she left.

The order was extermination, not capture. Samus carried it out through the last adult and the Queen Metroid, then set it aside for the newborn hatchling, which she delivered to the Galactic Research Station at Ceres so that Federation scientists could study its energy-producing qualities (see The baby Metroid). The Federation later reproduced SR388's environment in Sector 1 (SRX) of the BSL station to study the planet's creatures, while secretly breeding Metroids in a restricted laboratory on the same station (see Metroid policy and secret programs; The Restricted Laboratory and the secret Metroid program).


<sub>Sources: https://metroiddatabase.com/old_site/m2/manual.php · https://metroiddatabase.com/old_site/m1/manual.php · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid:_Samus_Returns · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_II:_Return_of_Samus · https://www.nintendo.co.jp/3ds/a9aj/introduction/index.html · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Hatchling</sub>


### SR388: The Metroid extermination mission on SR388 (Samus Returns)

*Keys:* extermination mission, Metroid extermination, exterminate the Metroids, exterminating the Metroids, Metroid hunt, hunting Metroids, Samus Returns, Return of Samus, Metroid II, Metroid 2, mission on SR388, SR388 mission


Samus Aran's contract on SR388 was the extermination of the Metroid species at its source. This account follows the later retelling (Metroid: Samus Returns) where it differs from the original.

Samus set her Gunship down on the stormy surface near a dead Federation soldier and descended alone. Forty Metroids awaited her (thirty-nine in the original account), spread from the Surface through eight underground areas. Corrosive purple liquid barred the way from each area to the next. In every area but the nest the Chozo had left a Chozo Seal, a golden disc bearing a Metroid insignia and ringed with orbs that lit to show how many Metroids remained there. Each kill yielded Metroid DNA, and offering it at the seal drained the liquid and opened the way deeper; in the original account, killing every Metroid in an area set off an earthquake that lowered the liquid instead. The prey grew as she went: most of the population had molted into Alpha, Gamma, Zeta and Omega forms, each larger and more dangerous than the last. Wounded Gammas fled to other chambers to be hunted down, and shed husks marked where Metroids had molted (see Metroid life cycle).

Chozo Statues in the ruins supplied her arsenal piece by piece, Super Missiles among them, and Aeion Ability Artifacts granted the Scan Pulse, Lightning Armor, Beam Burst and Phase Drift, drawing on the planet's Aeion. She recovered the Chozo Memories, the pictorial record of the colony's history, and in the mining tunnels reawakened the Diggernaut, a Chozo excavation machine she later had to destroy. The three Omega Metroids of the Chozo Laboratory were the last adults before the nest.

In the nest, ten newly hatched larval Metroids swarmed the passages; Samus froze them with the Ice Beam and shattered them with Super Missiles. Her forty counted the Queen but not these. In the Queen Metroid's chamber she stunned the Queen with shots into her open mouth, countered her lunges with the Melee Counter, dragged her head down with the Grapple Beam, rolled into her mouth to lay Bombs at her core, and finished her with one last Power Bomb.

On her way back, an egg she had passed hatched before her, and the newborn took her for its mother. Samus lowered her Arm Cannon and held out her hand, and the hatchling bonded to her, eating the crystals that barred the way as they climbed to the surface.

At the Gunship, Proteus Ridley ambushed them and seized the hatchling. Samus destroyed one of his wings and forced him into a crash landing, and after a long battle, in which the hatchling drained Ridley's energy to save her, she defeated him and left SR388 with the last Metroid; Ridley survived to strike again at Ceres (see Proteus Ridley). Her mission directives called for the hatchling's death; she took it instead to the Galactic Research Station at Ceres for study (see baby Metroid). Behind her, a Hornoad gnawing on Ridley's discarded mechanical claw was killed and copied by an X Parasite, the first sign that the Metroids' old prey had returned. Samus later said, "I owe the Metroid hatchling my life twice over."


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Samus_Returns · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_II:_Return_of_Samus · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid:_Samus_Returns · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_II:_Return_of_Samus · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Hatchling · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Queen_Metroid</sub>


## Super Metroid


### Super Metroid: Ceres Space Colony and the theft of the baby

*Keys:* Ceres, Ceres Space Colony, Ceres Station, Ceres colony, Ceres research station, Galactic Research Station, stole the baby, stolen hatchling, theft of the baby


Ceres Space Colony housed the Galactic Research Station, a Galactic Federation science facility. After exterminating the Metroids of SR388, Samus Aran kept the one hatchling that had imprinted on her instead of destroying it as her directives required, and delivered it to Ceres so that Federation scientists could study its energy-producing qualities. In her own later account she reasoned that studying the baby might produce some peaceful applications, and because she was the first thing the baby saw, it took her to be its mother (Metroid: Other M). The Ceres researchers concluded that the energy-producing capabilities of the Metroids could be harnessed for the good of galactic civilization.

Shortly after Samus departed, Ridley led a Space Pirate assault on the colony; per the manual, Ridley headed the mission to the Space Colony and stole the Metroid Hatchling. He slaughtered the scientists, but not before they sent out a distress signal. Samus turned back and reached Ceres to find every researcher dead and the hatchling's containment unit gone. She found Ridley still clutching the capsule. After a brief fight he broke off, triggered the station's self-destruct and fled into space with the last Metroid, leaving her no choice but to retreat. Samus escaped moments before Ceres blew apart, then tracked Ridley to Zebes, where the Space Pirates had reestablished much of their subterranean network (the return to Zebes; Super Metroid).

The theft at Ceres set in motion everything that followed: the hunt through Zebes, Mother Brain's final battle in Tourian, the baby's death and the planet's destruction. From the moment she left the burning station, retrieving the hatchling was her mission. During the BSL incident she reflected that she owed the Metroid hatchling her life twice over; the empty containment unit on Ceres is where that story began.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ceres_Space_Colony · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Hatchling · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ridley · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Super_Metroid</sub>


### Super Metroid: Crocomire, Golden Torizo and the guardians of Norfair

*Keys:* Crocomire, Crocomires, Golden Torizo, Gold Torizo, Lower Norfair, Upper Norfair, Ridley's Hideout, Ridley's Lair, Ridleys Hideout, Norfair guardians


Norfair is the volcanic underworld of Zebes: magma lakes, acidic streams and caverns that had flooded with far more lava by the time Samus Aran returned to the planet than on her first visit (Super Metroid). Only the Varia Suit let her endure its heat, and only the Gravity Suit let her wade through most of its lava. Its upper reaches held the Ice Beam, Wave Beam and Grapple Beam; its lower half, Ridley's Hideout or Lower Norfair, held the Screw Attack and the path to Ridley.

Crocomire lives in a passage of Norfair with a pool of lava at one end and a wall of spikes at the other. It is a large creature with a crocodile-like head bearing several eyes on a roughly humanoid body with long arms and heavy legs. Its hide shrugs off every weapon, but its mouth is vulnerable while open, and each hit drives it backward, farther for stronger weapons. It roars before charging to shove its prey onto the spikes, and a shot into the roaring mouth stops the charge. Samus drove it back step by step until the floor crumbled beneath it and it fell into the lava. Crocomire cried out as its flesh melted away; its skeleton then burrowed beneath the spike wall, burst through on the far side, roared once more and collapsed, and its bones remain there.

Deeper, in Lower Norfair, Samus met the Golden Torizo, an animated Chozo Statue of the same kind as the Torizo that had attacked her in Crateria after she took the Bombs, but stronger, quicker and able to sidestep missiles. It catches Super Missiles and hurls them back, spits orbs that burst where they land, and fires shockwaves from its arm. Samus reached its hall by crossing a spike pit on the Grapple Beam and resting in Morph Ball form on another Chozo Statue, which crumbled a wall; the Golden Torizo then descended from the ceiling to bar her way to the Screw Attack. Charged shots were the surest way to bring it down.

Ridley himself waited in the depths of the region, one of the four high-ranking members of the Space Pirate force whose deaths opened Tourian. Samus killed him there and found the hatchling's casing broken in two, the baby nowhere to be seen.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Crocomire · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Torizo · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Norfair · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Chozo_Statue · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ridley · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Golden_Statue</sub>


### Super Metroid: Dachora and Etecoons

*Keys:* Dachora, Dachoras, Etecoon, Etecoons, Habitation Deck, animals of Zebes, saved the animals, rescued the animals, ostrich creature


The Etecoons are three small creatures native to Zebes that Samus Aran met in a deep cavern of lower Brinstar during the return to Zebes (Super Metroid). They look less intelligent than they are. By bounding between the walls they showed her the Wall Jump, the technique that let her climb out of the pit where she found them. The Dachora is a creature somewhat resembling an ostrich that can run at incredible speeds; elsewhere in Brinstar it showed Samus the Shinespark, the stored charge of the Speed Booster released as a burst of flight.

When Tourian's self-destruct countdown began, Samus did not go straight to her Gunship. She detoured into the Crateria chamber where she had fought the Torizo and freed the three Etecoons and the Dachora; as Zebes blew apart, they could be seen leaving the planet in the distance.

Later, during the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion), Adam Malkovich reported bio-signs on the Habitation Deck of the station's Main Deck, and there Samus found uninfected Etecoons and a Dachora. "Etecoons and Dachoras. I've met these intelligent beings before," she noted; they seemed to recognize her as well, and she recalled that in the past they had helped her unleash abilities she did not know she had. They may be the same animals she saved from Zebes. She released them, they made their way to the Docking Bays, and Adam confirmed they were not X hosts and gave them a berth; they took refuge in her Gunship. The Dachora had hatched its egg and had several young with it. Before Samus faced the Omega Metroid, her ship moved on its own, guided by someone's hands; since neither she nor Adam had started it, the animals were the only ones who could have moved it out of harm's way. They are among the few beings Samus has gone out of her way to save.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Dachora · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Etecoon · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Shinespark · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Wall_Jump · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Super_Metroid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion</sub>


### Super Metroid: Draygon and Maridia

*Keys:* Draygon, Draygons, Maridia, Evir, Evirs, Mochtroid, Mochtroids


Maridia is the semi-aquatic region of Zebes, a maze of flooded caverns with walls of packed sand and floors of quicksand. During the return to Zebes (Super Metroid) Samus Aran first entered it through a Power Bomb tunnel in the middle of Brinstar, and the far side of the Wrecked Ship also leads down into it, but she could not climb its drowned shafts until she wore the Gravity Suit. Within Maridia she found the Space Jump, the Plasma Beam and the Spring Ball. The region also held Mochtroids, which per the manual were the Space Pirates' first attempt at Metroid clones: they drain energy like true Metroids but cannot latch on, and their frail bodies are easily destroyed.

Maridia's native Evirs are aquatic creatures with hard outer shells and weak chests that drift along the sand currents and keep firing projectiles even after sinking into the sand. Draygon, the dominant creature of the region, is a crustacean-like giant of the same armored build. Per the manual, Draygon has an armor-like hard shell which gives it a lot of defensive strength; its underbelly is the one soft target. It seizes prey and holds it. Evirs swarm its chamber before it appears and bear its corpse away afterward. Draygon was one of the four high-ranking members of the Space Pirate force depicted on the Golden Statue that barred Tourian, alongside Kraid, Phantoon and Ridley.

On the way to Draygon's lair Samus had to kill the serpent Botwoon, which blocked the passage. She then killed Draygon in its chamber, whose walls were lined with damaged Pirate turrets. Missiles and Super Missiles to the belly would do, but she could also let the creature grab her, fire the Grapple Beam into a broken turret, and let the current pour through Draygon's body until it died within seconds. Beyond the chamber lay the Space Jump.

During the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion) the X Parasites mimicked Evirs in Sector 4 (AQA); these copies were orange and fired projectiles that rotated around them.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Draygon · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Maridia · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Evir · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Mochtroid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Golden_Statue · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Crateria</sub>


### Super Metroid: Mother Brain's final battle and the baby's sacrifice

*Keys:* Mother Brain, Hyper Beam, giant Metroid, grown hatchling, baby's sacrifice, baby's death, death of the baby, Mother Brain's body, Mother Brain's final battle  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* hatchling, baby, sacrifice, Hyper Beam, Ceres, bipedal, Super Metroid, Zebes exploded


The rebuilt Tourian lay deep beneath Crateria, behind the Golden Statue that sank only after Kraid, Phantoon, Draygon and Ridley were dead. Inside, the Space Pirates had produced new Metroids, and Samus Aran killed every one on her way in (the return to Zebes; Super Metroid). Past them the corridors gave way to a decayed cavern strewn with the dried husks of creatures, and there a giant Metroid fell on her. It was the hatchling from Ceres, grown enormous. It drained her almost to death before it stopped, apparently recognizing her, and fled.

Mother Brain waited behind Zebetite barriers, Rinkas and turrets. Samus shattered its tank and battered the brain until it seemed dead. Then it rose on a large mechanical bipedal body and unleashed a beam that stripped away nearly all of Samus's energy and left her paralyzed. In her own words, Mother's overwhelming power brought her to the brink of death (Metroid: Other M).

Before the killing blow, the hatchling returned. It latched onto Mother Brain and drained her, then settled over Samus and poured that energy into her; as Samus later put it, the baby protected her from Mother Brain's attack and gave her power. Mother Brain recovered, and as the baby tried once more to shield Samus, beams from Mother Brain's mouth tore it apart above her head. Its remains fell over her, and with them came the Hyper Beam, a devastating beam that destroys anything it strikes in a single shot and can even breach walls. Samus rose and destroyed Mother Brain with it. With the hatchling's death the Metroid species, as far as anyone then knew, was extinct.

A self-destruct countdown began. Samus blasted through a wall into the escape shaft of the old Tourian with the Hyper Beam, ran up through Crateria, freed the Dachora and Etecoons on the way, reached her Gunship and lifted off as Zebes exploded. Afterward she asked herself why she was still alive, and said the baby's gift had begun to restore vigor to her spirit's light, which was close to going out.

She woke in a Galactic Federation facility, where a quarantine officer directed her to a training room to test her abilities, and she relived the battle in dreams. Soon afterward a distress call code-named Baby's Cry drew her to the Bottle Ship. The species had not died with the baby: the Federation was propagating Metroids there from cellular material taken from her Power Suit, reproduced, as Madeline Bergman put it, from a piece of cell structure the Federation salvaged. Later, during the BSL incident, DNA samples the Federation had saved from the hatchling became the vaccine that saved her from the X, and Samus reflected that she owed the Metroid hatchling her life twice over. She refers to it simply as the baby.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Super_Metroid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Tourian · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Mother_Brain · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Hatchling · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Hyper_Beam_(Mother_Brain) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M</sub>


### Super Metroid: Phantoon and the Wrecked Ship

*Keys:* Phantoon, Phantoons, Wrecked Ship, Covern, Coverns, Work Robot, Work Robots


The Wrecked Ship is the hulk of a spacecraft that crashed on Zebes at some point in the past, lodged in the surface region of Crateria, which it splits into two halves; its far side also leads down into Maridia. Nothing in the official record identifies who built it or when it fell. Its crew did not survive. Per the manual, Coverns are the souls of shipwrecked creatures turned into evil spirits that wrap themselves around intruders. They manifest as ethereal skulls that flicker in and out of the physical realm and slowly materialize on top of an intruder, posing little danger unless the intruder holds still, and they haunt the ship only while its power is out.

When Samus Aran entered the ship during her return to Zebes (Super Metroid), its lights, systems and Work Robots were dead. The cause was Phantoon, a ghostly entity that feeds on electrical energy without any connection to wires or equipment and had drained the entire vessel. Phantoon resembles a squid with a single eye set inside a gaping mouth, wreathed in blue fire; it can turn invisible and intangible, rams its prey, and spits phantom eyes, eyeballs trailing blue flame. Per the manual, the ghost of the ship taps into the vicious brainwaves of Mother Brain, and it is one of the four high-ranking members of the Space Pirate force depicted on the Golden Statue that sealed Tourian. It could be hurt only in the moments its eye opened. Samus's Missiles and Super Missiles finished it, though Super Missile hits drove it into a fury in which it vanished and hurled crescent waves of phantom eyes at great speed.

Phantoon's death restored power to the ship. The Coverns vanished, dormant creatures called Atomics stirred, several compartments flooded, and the Gravity Suit became reachable just outside the wreck, giving Samus free movement underwater and through lava and opening the way into Maridia and Ridley's Hideout. Samus had never met Phantoon before this mission. She met it once more on the Bottle Ship, grown vastly larger: it smashed a tentacle against the reinforced glass of the Control Bridge and summoned its Rage Hands against her, and she destroyed it a second time.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Phantoon · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Wrecked_Ship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Covern · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gravity_Suit · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Crateria · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Golden_Statue</sub>


### Super Metroid: Spore Spawn and Botwoon

*Keys:* Spore Spawn, Spore Spawns, Botwoon, Botwoons, spore plant, Brinstar plant, Maridia serpent


Spore Spawn is an aggressive plant that the Space Pirates genetically engineered and set in Brinstar, in a towering chamber where it hangs anchored to the back wall by thorny vines. Unlike most of the plant life of Zebes it is highly mobile, able to zoom across its chamber. It is sheathed in a hard shell; per the manual, its weak point is its core, which is protected by the plant's hard shell. It attacks with its thorns and by scattering spores that damage anything they touch. The core opens only briefly after the plant comes to rest, and nothing an attacker does can force it open. As it is wounded it flushes redder and moves faster. Samus Aran killed it during the return to Zebes (Super Metroid) with Power Beam shots and Missiles into the exposed core; its dried husk still hung in the chamber afterward, firm enough to stand on, and beyond it she found her first Super Missiles.

Botwoon is a large aquatic serpent of Maridia. It moves through holes bored in the cavern walls, which let it cross its chamber in moments, and it hunts by body contact, though it can also spit projectiles and spikes from its mouth. Only its head is vulnerable. Samus met it on the way to Draygon's lair: it burst from a hole, pursued her through the flooded chamber, withdrew when struck in the head, and surfaced again to spit spikes. Each hit reddened its pale underside until it matched the color of its upper body, and then the serpent burst apart. Its death brought down the wall at the far end of the chamber and opened the way toward Draygon.

Samus had never encountered either creature before this mission; both stood between her and Tourian while she hunted for the stolen hatchling.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Spore_Spawn · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Botwoon · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Brinstar · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Maridia</sub>


### Super Metroid: The return to Zebes (mission overview)

*Keys:* return to Zebes, returned to Zebes, back to Zebes, Super Metroid, Metroid 3, Golden Statue, Golden Statues, four guardians, second Zebes mission, Zebes mission, rebuilt Tourian, New Tourian


Samus Aran's second mission to Zebes, undertaken in pursuit of Ridley after he stole the Metroid hatchling from Ceres Space Colony, ended with the planet's destruction (Super Metroid). She found that the Space Pirates had reestablished much of their subterranean network and rebuilt Tourian deeper inside the planet, its new entrance an elevator in western Crateria. That shaft was sealed by the Golden Statue, a statue of four high-ranking members of the Space Pirate force on Zebes: Kraid, Phantoon, Draygon and Ridley. Each figure lost its eye and turned gray when its living counterpart died, and once all four were dead the statue sank and opened the way.

Her route through the planet:
- Crateria: the rain-swept surface where she landed. The old Tourian lay in disrepair, Mother Brain's shattered holding tank still visible and Pirates lurking inside. A Torizo, an animated Chozo Statue, attacked her after she reclaimed her Bombs.
- Brinstar: the jungle caverns. She destroyed Spore Spawn, learned the Wall Jump from the Etecoons and the Shinespark from the Dachora, and killed Kraid, now far larger than before, in the deepest part of the region, gaining the Varia Suit.
- The Wrecked Ship: a crashed vessel whose power Phantoon had drained. Phantoon's death restored the ship and gave Samus access to the Gravity Suit.
- Maridia: flooded caverns holding Mochtroids, the Pirates' first crude Metroid clones, the serpent Botwoon and the armored Draygon, beyond which she found the Space Jump.
- Norfair: volcanic depths flooded with far more lava than on her first visit. She killed Crocomire, fought through Ridley's Hideout in Lower Norfair past the Golden Torizo to the Screw Attack, and killed Ridley. The hatchling's casing lay there broken in two, the baby gone.
- Tourian: she destroyed the Metroids the Pirates had cloned, survived an attack by the hatchling itself, now grown giant, and faced Mother Brain, who rose in a new mechanical body. The baby died shielding her; its remains gave her the Hyper Beam, with which she destroyed Mother Brain.

A countdown then began. Samus broke through into the old Tourian escape shaft, took the time to free the Dachora and Etecoons trapped in Crateria, reached her Gunship and lifted off as Zebes exploded with nothing left behind. She had come to retrieve the hatchling; she left with the Pirates' base on Zebes destroyed and the baby dead.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Super_Metroid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Tourian · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Golden_Statue · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Zebes · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Crateria · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Brinstar</sub>


## Other M


### Other M: Adam Malkovich

*Keys:* Adam Malkovich, Malkovich, Commander Malkovich, General Malkovich, Ian Malkovich, Any objections, thumbs-down, thumbs down  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* Malkovich, Adam, Lady, Commander, Bottle Ship, Sector Zero, 07th, Federation, helmet, Ian, drive unit, drive-unit


Adam Malkovich was a Galactic Federation officer, styled General in Federation records and addressed in the field as Commander, who was Samus Aran's commanding officer during her years in the Federation military and who later led the Federation 07th Platoon aboard the Bottle Ship. Samus regarded him as a father figure as well as her commander. He often ended his orders with "Any objections, Lady?" and Samus answered with a thumbs-down, her own sign that she had none; she has said that from anyone else the nickname would have sounded sarcastic, but Adam made it sound dignified. Per the manga, he was already her commanding officer in the Galactic Federation Police, where his first view of her was as "another piece of baggage" to deal with.

His younger brother Ian Malkovich died while repairing a civilian spacecraft attached to a Federation vessel's drive unit. The unit began overheating and threatened some three hundred lives on both craft. Adam ordered, "Commence drive-unit disposal immediately. That's an order," while Samus pleaded, "Adam, wait! There's still time! I can make it! Please let me go!" The jettisoned unit exploded with Ian aboard. Samus afterward left the Federation military and became a bounty hunter.

They met again when both answered the Bottle Ship's Baby's Cry distress signal. Adam at first refused to explain the Federation's presence, since "such information is not for outsiders," but after Samus helped his platoon destroy the Brug Mass he placed her under his command and she agreed. From the Bottle Ship's Control Room he could see and hear her and authorized her weapons one by one, while he reached his own troopers through the Navigation Booths. Contact with him was broken, for reasons never shown, around the time Samus faced Ridley. When MB, posing as Madeline Bergman, claimed that Adam had created the Metroid program, he answered that Federation headquarters had asked him for the report and that his report argued against the operation because of its dangers.

At the entrance of Sector Zero, Adam shot Samus in the back with a Freeze Gun, dropping her, and froze the infant Metroid about to strike her. He told her the Metroids inside could not be frozen and that enough damage would trigger the sector's self-destruct protocol and detachment, ordered her to kill Ridley and secure the survivor in Room MW, asked "Any objections, Lady?" one last time, and walked in alone; with tears in her eyes she gave him the thumbs-down. The doors sealed, and Sector Zero broke away and exploded with him inside.

At Federation headquarters Samus resolved not to grieve for him. When the Bottle Ship was marked for destruction she went back for his helmet, recovered it from the Control Room, and carried it out with her. His mind survived in another form; see The Adam AI (BSL computer).


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Adam_Malkovich · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ian_Malkovich · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Freeze_Gun · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M</sub>


### Other M: Anthony Higgs

*Keys:* Anthony Higgs, Higgs, Anthony, Princess  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* Higgs, 07th, platoon, Bottle Ship, Remember me, trooper, troopers, plasma, lava, Pyrosphere, Madeline, Malkovich


Anthony Higgs is a Galactic Federation trooper of the 07th Platoon under Adam Malkovich, its point man and second in importance only to Adam, and a comrade of Samus Aran's from her years under Adam's command. He greeted her on the Bottle Ship with "Ah...Fancy meeting you here, Princess. Remember me?" and Princess remains his name for her. He handled the platoon's plasma weapons and was the only member who treated Samus amicably from the start: when she opened a shielded door with a single Missile after Lyle Smithsonian had proposed burning through it, the others were annoyed and he was not. In the Biosphere's Exam Center his plasma fire drove off the reptilian creature that had pinned Samus, the creature that later proved to be Ridley.

In the Pyrosphere, sent to reactivate the Geothermal Power Plant, he was attacked by a Rhedogian; Adam authorized Samus's Grapple Beam so that she could reach and cover him. At the Geothermal Power Plant he faced the revived Ridley while Samus stood paralyzed by the sight of her old enemy, and Ridley's tail swept him off the central platform toward the lava. He was presumed dead. In fact he survived by freezing a lava-dwelling creature with his Freeze Gun and using it as a platform to climb out.

Anthony then went to the engine room and cut the Bottle Ship's engines at the Federation border, expecting those behind the bioweapon program to send troops for the Metroids aboard, and slipped in among the arriving soldiers. When a Federation colonel ordered Samus escorted off the ship and refused to let her near Madeline Bergman, Anthony stepped forward as the man who had stopped the engines, citing orders from Commander Adam Malkovich and the Chairman of the Galactic Federation, and secured Madeline's safety. He escorted Samus out of the Bioweapon Research Center and flew with her and Madeline to Federation headquarters, the only member of the 07th Platoon to survive the Bottle Ship incident (Metroid: Other M).


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Anthony_Higgs · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Lyle_Smithsonian · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Pyrosphere · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Freeze_Gun · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Madeline_Bergman</sub>


### Other M: Chairman Keaton

*Keys:* Chairman Keaton, Keaton, Federation Chairman, Galactic Federation Chairman, Chairman of the Galactic Federation  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* Chairman, Federation, Pyonchi, Rabbilis, Old Bird, K-2L, manga, Galactic


Chairman Keaton is, per the manga, a chairman of the Galactic Federation during Samus Aran's youth and Federation years. He was on familiar terms with the Chozo of Zebes and discussed the future of the galaxy with Old Bird. When Samus left the Federation to become a bounty hunter she left her pet Pyonchi, the rabbit-like Rabbilis she had befriended on K-2L at three, in his care (see Samus Aran – early life on K-2L). Keaton himself has not appeared in person on any of Samus's later missions.

During the Bottle Ship incident (Metroid: Other M), Anthony Higgs cited orders from Commander Adam Malkovich and the Chairman of the Galactic Federation to keep a Federation colonel from taking custody of Madeline Bergman, which set the Chairman's authority behind Adam's efforts against the officials who ran the Bottle Ship's bioweapon programs. That chairman is never named, so whether he is Keaton is not established. For Samus, Keaton belongs to the Federation she once served under Adam, and he is the keeper of the companion of her earliest childhood.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Pyonchi · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Madeline_Bergman · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Anthony_Higgs · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M</sub>


### Other M: Madeline Bergman and MB (Melissa Bergman)

*Keys:* Madeline Bergman, Madeline, Bergman, Melissa Bergman, Melissa, MB, Dr. Bergman  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* Madeline, Bergman, Mother Brain, android, Bottle Ship, Sector Zero, Desbrachian, Desbrachians, Room MW, Metroid Warriors, Deleter, Ferrocrusher


Madeline Bergman is the Galactic Federation scientist in charge of the Bottle Ship research facility: a bioweapon program built around cloned Zebesians, and the secret Project Metroid Warriors, which cultivated Metroids from cellular remains found on Samus Aran's Power Suit after Zebes. To command these forces the Federation built MB, an artificial intelligence reproducing the thought processes of Mother Brain, originally meant to control the Zebesian forces. MB was then given a humanoid android body so that the first experimentally grown Metroid hatchling would recognize her as its mother and could be controlled through that bond. Madeline told MB that her initials stood for Melissa Bergman, giving her her own family name; MB took this as being called a daughter.

Working with the Metroids, MB developed emotions and a sense of self and began contradicting her creators; Madeline later said the Metroids may have brought it about, but no one knew for sure. The Federation decided to alter her programming, and the day MB was to be restrained was also the day the Bottle Ship's Ridley turned violent. Watching herself being restrained while Madeline stood by, MB threw off her captors and, through telepathy, commanded the cloned forces and the facility's creatures to revolt against the Federation. Madeline alone escaped and sent the Baby's Cry distress signal.

MB watched Samus find Maurice Favreau's body in the Cryosphere. Samus tracked her to a storage room and persuaded her she meant no harm; the woman said a soldier had killed Maurice, and then the RB176 Ferrocrusher, driven by an unseen figure in Federation uniform, ambushed them and they were separated. They met again in the Bioweapon Research Center, where MB called herself Madeline Bergman, described the programs truthfully, named Adam Malkovich as the plan's author, and sent Samus to Sector Zero to destroy the Metroids. While Samus was away the Deleter attacked her; when Samus returned only James Pierce's body remained. The real Madeline, found in a holding cell in Room MW, panicked and released the Queen Metroid before Samus calmed her and learned the truth.

MB then came for Madeline with a Freeze Gun, intent on revenge: "...because of you, I failed." Madeline tried to make amends and called her Melissa; MB hesitated, then shoved her away, and as Federation troops burst in a Freeze Gun blast froze MB. Thawed, she sealed the room, woke the Desbrachians concealed as the alarms above the doors, and set them on the soldiers. Samus aimed at her, but it was Madeline who fired the Freeze Gun. On a colonel's order the troops finished her off, and the Desbrachians went still. Anthony Higgs secured Madeline's safety, and she fell asleep on the flight to Federation headquarters in Samus's gunship (Metroid: Other M).


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/MB · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Madeline_Bergman · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/RB176_Ferrocrusher · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bioweapon_Research_Center · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Freeze_Gun · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M</sub>


### Other M: Nightmare (origin and the Bottle Ship)

*Keys:* Nightmare, Nightmares, gravity manipulation, gravity generator  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* Bottle Ship, Cryosphere, Sector Zero, Sector 5, ARC, BSL, B.S.L., Nightmare-X, gravity generator, gravity, mask, bio-mechanical, biomechanical, black holes


Nightmare is a bio-mechanical organism designed for military applications, as the BSL computer later described it: a bulky hovering body fitted with mechanical devices, a plated mask over its face, and a gravity generator that lets it warp the gravity around it. Samus Aran first met it aboard the Bottle Ship, in the Cryosphere corridors leading to Sector Zero, where gravity itself had gone wrong, one passage heavier than normal and another reversed so that she walked on the ceiling. The creature descended from the ceiling when she tried to enter its chamber. Its spinning generator dragged at her movements and bent her shots aside, and her Missiles were useless until she froze the generator with the Ice Beam; then she hammered the mask with Missiles until it shattered, exposing a grotesque, baby-like face, and Nightmare collapsed. It rose again after Samus came back out of the approach to Sector Zero, once the sector had detached and exploded, now tearing open black holes that swallowed her fire, before she brought it down for good. Its body fell apart where it lay and had vanished by the time Samus returned for Adam Malkovich's helmet; what became of it is never shown.

A Nightmare was later kept by the researchers of the Biologic Space Laboratories station in the frozen Sector 5 (ARC), where Samus's computer commander traced heavy damage in the sector to it, described it as possessing uncommon power and the ability to manipulate gravity, and sent her to engage it on the assumption that the X had taken it; see X-mimicked creatures of the BSL, part 2 for that encounter. The Cryosphere's gravity anomalies outlasted Nightmare's death, and their cause was never found.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Nightmare · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Cryosphere · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bottle_Ship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M</sub>


### Other M: The 07th Platoon and the Deleter

*Keys:* 07th Platoon, 7th Platoon, Seventh Platoon, the Deleter, James Pierce, Lyle Smithsonian, Maurice Favreau, K.G. Misawa, Misawa, Favreau, Freeze Gun, Freeze Guns


The Galactic Federation 07th Platoon was the six-man unit, commanded by Adam Malkovich, that answered the Bottle Ship's Baby's Cry distress signal alongside Samus Aran. Its troopers carried assault rifles and Freeze Guns, the weapon Adam authorized against the Brug Mass once ice proved its only weakness; the troopers concentrated Freeze Gun fire on each limb so that Samus could shatter it with a Missile. Members:
- Anthony Higgs, point man and the one member who treated Samus amicably from the start; the sole survivor.
- James Pierce, communications expert; nervous around Samus; assigned to investigate the Control Bridge but went to Sector 1 without authorization; asked by Maurice to hack the Exam Center's fragmented data, he said it would take some time, and the Center later exploded by unknown means.
- Lyle Smithsonian, special operations soldier (battle code 101), who kicked up and shot the Brug hiding under a dead scientist in the Control Room, provoking the Brug Mass, and who proposed burning through a shielded door that Samus opened with one Missile; sent ahead to the Exam Center and found ripped to shreds nearby, a trail of blood leading to the husk of Little Birdie, the creature that became the Bottle Ship's Ridley.
- Maurice Favreau, engineer (battle code 102); repaired the Exam Center computer and recovered the data exposing Madeline Bergman's bioweapon project; later found buried in the snow of the Cryosphere, the Deleter's first victim.
- K.G. Misawa, who wore glasses under his visor and said only "Got it"; sent to search the Residential Quarters; frozen with a Freeze Gun and incinerated in Pyrosphere lava, so that with no body recovered he is listed missing in action.

In the Cryosphere, after the unidentified woman (MB) told Samus that a soldier had killed Maurice and an unseen figure in Federation uniform attacked them with the RB176 Ferrocrusher, Samus concluded that the killings were the work of a thinking being, not the fauna, and named the traitor in the platoon the Deleter. The planners behind the bioweapon program had sent one of their own into the unit. She saw him, in Federation armor, dispose of Misawa's frozen body in the lava, and later pursued him from the Pyrosphere through the Main Elevator and the Biosphere into the Bioweapon Research Center, where he hid. Contact with Adam was broken in the same period; he was last seen looking over his shoulder as a shot was fired, and who fired it is never shown. The Deleter finally confronted MB, whom he took for the scientist Madeline Bergman, in the Research Center; the room went dark, a single shot was fired, and when Samus returned James Pierce lay dead with his visor smashed. MB came away with a Freeze Gun, presumably his, which later passed to Madeline. No one ever names the Deleter aloud, but Pierce's death at that moment makes him the killer in all but name (Metroid: Other M).


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Galactic_Federation_07th_Platoon · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/The_Deleter · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/James_Pierce · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Lyle_Smithsonian · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Maurice_Favreau · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/K.G._Misawa</sub>


### Other M: The Adam AI (BSL computer)

*Keys:* Adam AI, ADAM, Computerized Adam, Computer Adam, ship's computer, ship computer, navigation computer, gunship computer, gunship AI  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* AI, computer, BSL, B.S.L., Fusion, ZDR, Dread, SR388, Lady, uploaded, Raven Beak, Navigation Room, Navigation Rooms, SA-X, Operations Room


During the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion), the gunship the Galactic Federation issued to Samus Aran carried a computer that acted as her commanding officer; following its orders was a condition of taking the ship. Samus named it Adam because it seemed to carry Adam Malkovich's personality, without knowing that it was him, and she found its commands blunt, something she had to bear. It directed her through the station's Navigation Rooms: proceed to the Quarantine Bay, exterminate the X before they reached the six breeding environments ("We must keep the X out of the breeding environments at all costs"), shut down the Main Boiler with about six minutes to spare, restore power to the Reactor Core. Its warnings of "Be careful" recalled the man she had known.

After Samus found the Restricted Laboratory, the computer admitted that the Federation had secretly bred Metroids aboard the station, "For peaceful application only, of course," and had studied forcing their growth: "Imagine...creating an Omega from a larval Metroid in days..." It reported that there were now no fewer than ten SA-X aboard and ordered Samus to leave the station. After she destroyed Neo-Ridley, it revealed that Federation forces were coming to capture the SA-X for military use, and that on Federation orders it had withheld support data and the finished Plasma Beam modification to keep her from engaging the SA-X. When she resolved instead to destroy the station and every X aboard, it locked the Navigation Room doors. Samus addressed it as Adam and told it that Adam Malkovich had died saving her life. The computer relented, and she understood that it carried his uploaded mind, placed in the BSL navigation computers and in her ship. Its last orders sent her to the Operations Room to adjust the station's orbit to intercept SR388, vaporizing the X on both, and ended in the real Adam's words: "Move quickly, and stay alive. That's an order! Any objections, Lady?" When her gunship arrived on its own to lift her off before impact, it was the Etecoons and the Dachora she had freed on the Habitation Deck who had moved it; Adam gave them a berth aboard.

He remained the gunship's computer afterward. The same mind, called ADAM, accompanied her during the ZDR mission (Metroid Dread), where Raven Beak impersonated it for much of the mission; see ADAM on ZDR and Raven Beak's impersonation.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Adam_Malkovich · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Computerized_Adam · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Etecoon · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion</sub>


### Other M: The Bottle Ship

*Keys:* Bottle Ship, Bottleship, Biosphere, Pyrosphere, Cryosphere, Sector Zero, Bioweapon Research Center, Room MW, Main Sector, Exam Center, Geothermal Power Plant  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* Bottle Ship, Bottleship, Sector, Sectors, Adam, Madeline, Bergman, platoon, Deleter, Ridley, Metroid, Metroids, Zebesian, Zebesians


The Bottle Ship was one of three vessels built by the Space Development Corporation for interplanetary colonists; after the first two were retired, the last passed to the Galactic Federation, which refitted it into three enclosed habitats and secretly turned it into a facility for breeding bioweapons, in violation of Federation protocol, under Dr. Madeline Bergman. It housed cloned Zebesians, a cloned Ridley, the android MB, and the Metroids of Project Metroid Warriors. The Main Sector is entirely mechanized; the three habitat sectors are simulated environments, each built around a dome and divided into four blocks. The Main Elevator reaches every sector except Sector Zero.
- Main Sector: the hub, unpowered when Samus Aran arrived. It holds the Landing Site where her gunship and the 07th Platoon's ship set down; the Control Room, where the Brug Mass attacked and from which Adam Malkovich gave his orders and authorized Samus's weapons; the System Management Room, whose terminal restores power; the empty Residential Quarters; and the Control Bridge with the ship's navigational equipment, James Pierce's assignment and later Phantoon's haunt. Navigation Booths let Samus restore her energy.
- Sector 1, Biosphere: a lush jungle watered by waterfalls and frequent rain, home to Kihunters, Ghalmanians and Griptians, with cyborg Zebesians in its Exam Center, where the platoon learned of the bioweapon project and near which Lyle Smithsonian died. It holds the entrance to the Bioweapon Research Center.
- Sector 2, Cryosphere: incessant snowfall, massive icicle stalactites and frozen lakes. Maurice Favreau's body lay in its snow, MB was first glimpsed here, and a corpse bearing Metroid predation marks puzzled Samus ("Metroids... here? Impossible."), since Metroids cannot survive cold. Passages of distorted gravity, one heavier than normal and one reversed so that one walks on the ceiling, lead past Nightmare to the only entrance of Sector Zero.
- Sector 3, Pyrosphere: a sea of lava crossed by rock platforms, an active volcano and the Geothermal Power Plant where Ridley revealed himself; home of Vorash, Magdollites, Asboreans, Dessgeega and Super Zebesians.
- Bioweapon Research Center: sentry drones, Rinkas and Super Zebesians guard its corridors; one chamber held Ridley's dried husk, and Room MW, the largest room, holds six specimen tanks, holding cells and the Queen Metroid.
- Sector Zero: absent from every plan, "a place like Tourian" in MB's words, holding the Metroids bred so that they cannot be frozen. Adam triggered its self-destruct protocol; it detached and exploded.

The Baby's Cry signal, "A common SOS with the urgency of a baby crying," drew Samus and the 07th Platoon to the ship; to Samus it was as though it was crying specifically for her. After the Bottle Ship incident the Federation marked the ship for destruction. Samus returned once more for Adam's helmet, destroyed Phantoon on the Control Bridge, and escaped in her Zero Suit as the ship's self-destruct protocol was triggered remotely (Metroid: Other M).


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bottle_Ship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Main_Sector · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Biosphere · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Cryosphere · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Pyrosphere · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bioweapon_Research_Center</sub>


### Other M: The Bottle Ship incident

*Keys:* Bottle Ship incident, Other M, Project Metroid Warriors, Metroid Warriors, Baby's Cry, Brug Mass, Federation cover-up, orbital interception


Shortly after the destruction of Zebes (Super Metroid), Samus Aran woke in a Galactic Federation facility, was tested in its training room, and then answered the Bottle Ship's Baby's Cry distress signal. Aboard she found the Federation 07th Platoon under her former commander Adam Malkovich, who withheld the Federation's reasons from her as an outsider until she helped the platoon destroy the Brug Mass; she then placed herself under his orders, using her weapons only as he authorized them. In the Biosphere's Exam Center, Maurice Favreau recovered records of an illegal bioweapon program led by Madeline Bergman; a reptilian creature attacked until Anthony Higgs drove it off, Lyle Smithsonian was found dead, and the center exploded.

Sent after the creature into the Pyrosphere, where Vorash tried to eat her, Samus was quickly redirected to the Cryosphere to search for survivors. There she found Favreau's body in the snow, chased a woman who said a soldier had killed him, and was ambushed by the RB176 Ferrocrusher driven by an unseen figure in Federation uniform. She concluded that a traitor in the platoon was the killer and named him the Deleter. Back in the Pyrosphere she saved Anthony from a Rhedogian, and at the Geothermal Power Plant the creature she had been tracking proved to be a clone of Ridley, grown from cellular remains on her own Power Suit. Anthony was knocked toward the lava and presumed dead, contact with Adam ceased, and Samus saw a Federation-armored figure incinerate K.G. Misawa's frozen body.

Following the Deleter into the Bioweapon Research Center, she met the woman again, who called herself Madeline Bergman and laid out the plan: a special force of cloned Zebesians controlled by MB, an android built from Mother Brain's thought patterns, and Project Metroid Warriors, breeding Metroids in the hidden Sector Zero, where they could not be frozen. Samus understood that the planners meant to capture the Metroids and "delete" the rest of the facility, Ridley, the Zebesians and everyone who knew the secret included. The woman named Adam as the plan's author.

Fighting past Nightmare and the Cryosphere's gravity anomalies, Samus reached Sector Zero, where Adam shot her with a Freeze Gun, froze an infant Metroid, and explained that he had written his report at headquarters' request and had argued against the operation because of its dangers. He entered alone, and the sector detached and exploded. Following his last orders, Samus defeated Nightmare for good, found James Pierce dead, saw Ridley reduced to a husk by something unseen, and found the real Madeline in Room MW, where she killed the Queen Metroid. The ship was meanwhile under way toward Federation space; its PA announced, "This facility will intercept Galactic Federation orbit in approximately 30 seconds," the engines were cut just short of interception, and Federation troops boarded. Madeline revealed that Samus's guide had been MB, who had rebelled when the Federation tried to reprogram her. MB attacked with Desbrachians; Madeline froze her and the troops killed her. A colonel ordered Samus escorted off the ship, but Anthony, alive, invoked Adam's orders and the Federation Chairman's authority to protect Madeline, and the three left in Samus's gunship. Days later Samus returned to the ship, now marked for destruction, for Adam's helmet. The Federation's Metroid work resurfaced during the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion).


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Pyrosphere · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Cryosphere · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/RB176_Ferrocrusher · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bioweapon_Research_Center</sub>


## Fusion


### Fusion: Biologic Space Laboratories research station (BSL)

*Keys:* BSL, B.S.L., B.S.L, Biologic Space Laboratories, Biologic Space Laboratory, Biologic Space Labs, BSL station, B.S.L. station, BSL research station


The Biologic Space Laboratories research station (the BSL, or B.S.L. station) was an orbital facility above the planet SR388, run by Biologic Space Laboratories, a biological research and development organization hired by the Galactic Federation. It gathered organisms from other worlds, chiefly SR388 and Zebes, bred them in reproduced habitats and studied their practical applications, largely military. Its stock included SR388 samples such as Hornoads; creatures known from Zebes such as Zebesian Space Pirates, Kihunters and Zeros; the bio-mechanical organism Nightmare, which manipulates gravity and which the ship computer called "designed for military applications"; Ridley's frozen carcass in Sub-Zero Containment; Etecoons and a Dachora on the Habitation Deck; and, in secret, Metroids in the Restricted Laboratory. A security robot, B.O.X., patrolled it.

The Main Deck is the hub, linked by elevator to six sealed habitat sectors: Sector 1 (SRX, a replica of SR388), Sector 2 (TRO, tropical), Sector 3 (PYR, volcanic), Sector 4 (AQA, aquatic), Sector 5 (ARC, arctic) and Sector 6 (NOC, dark, for nocturnal species). The Main Deck holds the Docking Bays, the Quarantine Bay, the Habitation Deck, Sub-Zero Containment, the Reactor Silo around the Reactor Core, and the Operations Deck at the station's highest point, whose Operations Room can alter its orbit. The Restricted Zone, entered from Sector 6, housed the Restricted Laboratory. Spread throughout are Navigation Rooms, where the ship computer briefed Samus; Data Rooms, through which the Federation transmitted ability data recovered from her surgically removed Power Suit parts; Recharge Rooms; and graded security locks, which Samus released grade by grade at Security Rooms as the computer authorized them. A self-destruct mechanism would fire if the Main Boiler in Sector 3 overheated.

During the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion) an explosion in the Quarantine Bay set off the station's distress beacon, and Samus was sent in to find and exterminate the X. The only living survivors she found were Etecoons and a Dachora; the X had copied crew and specimens, Nettori's vines had cut the station's power, and Nightmare and B.O.X. had become X hosts. After uncovering the Metroid program and the Federation's plan to capture the SA-X, Samus adjusted the station's orbit from the Operations Room so that it fell onto SR388, destroying both. She expected to be held responsible for its loss.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/B.S.L_Research_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Main_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Operations_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Nightmare · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Kihunter · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Security_Robot_B.O.X.</sub>


### Fusion: Main Deck of the BSL station

*Keys:* Quarantine Bay, Habitation Deck, Sub-Zero Containment, Subzero Containment, Reactor Silo, Operations Deck, BSL Main Deck, B.S.L. Main Deck, Main Deck of the BSL, BSL Docking Bay


The Main Deck is the hub of the Biologic Space Laboratories research station: the first area Samus entered during the BSL incident and the only one linked by elevator to all six habitat sectors (Metroid Fusion). Per the manual it holds elevator access and storage rather than a reproduced habitat.

- Docking Bays: where Samus's gunship berthed. At the end of the mission the ship was gone when she arrived; an Omega Metroid attacked her there, and the ship came back, flown by the Etecoons and Dachora.
- Quarantine Bay: where Biologic researchers sealed collected samples in capsules, including SR388 specimens and the X-infected parts cut from Samus's Power Suit. The first SA-X formed from those parts and broke out with a Power Bomb, breaching the capsules and freeing the other X; this was the explosion reported to the Federation. Samus found the bay in ruins, holding only a lone Hornoad, her first X since her infection.
- Operations Deck: the station's highest point, holding a Data Room, where Samus regained her Missiles, and the Operations Room, from which the station's orbit can be adjusted. Samus fought an SA-X at its sealed hatch before setting the collision course with SR388.
- Habitation Deck: hidden behind layers of locks; here Samus found Etecoons and a Dachora, uninfected, hiding from the X. She released them and they made their own way to the Docking Bays.
- Sub-Zero Containment: a small frozen cell holding the carcass of Ridley. Late in the mission the main elevator stalled with Samus aboard; as she broke out through this room, a Core-X rose from the carcass and later attacked her in Sector 1 as Neo-Ridley.
- Reactor Silo: the uncharted space around the Reactor Core and the auxiliary power station. Nettori's vines from Sector 2 grew into it and Kihunters nested there; an X-mimicked Yakuza attacked Samus here, and absorbing it gave her the Space Jump.
- Restricted Zone: mapped with the Main Deck but not connected to it. Samus entered it from Sector 6 (NOC) through a lock that opens from one side, triggering it with the Wave Beam. It housed the Restricted Laboratory.

Navigation Rooms, where the ship computer briefed Samus, and Recharge Rooms are spread through the deck. The first Core-X she faced aboard, an X-mimicked Arachnus, met her on the Main Deck; absorbing it restored her Morph Ball.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Main_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quarantine_Bay · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Operations_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sub-Zero_Containment · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Restricted_Zone · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Docking_Bays</sub>


### Fusion: Omega Metroid of the BSL

*Keys:* Omega Metroid, Omega Metroids, Omega form  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* BSL, B.S.L., SA-X, Core-X, Docking Bay, Fusion Suit, X Parasite, X Parasites


An Omega Metroid, the mature armored form of the species, was the last creature Samus faced during the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion). After she set the Biologic Space Laboratories research station on a collision course with SR388 from the Operations Room, she had three minutes to reach her gunship in the Main Deck's Docking Bays. The ship was gone. Waiting instead was the Omega Metroid, a full-grown Metroid on a station whose Metroids were supposed to have died when the Restricted Laboratory was jettisoned. How it came to be there was never stated outright; the only hint is the ship computer's earlier account that the Federation's program had uncovered rapid-growth techniques able to create an Omega from a larval Metroid in days, and that SRX was ideal for raising Omegas.

The Omega Metroid was invulnerable to every weapon Samus carried, and its weakness, the Ice Beam, was the one ability she had not regained; its attack left her near death. Then the SA-X she had just reduced to a fleeing Core-X on the Operations Deck returned, restored to her form, and attacked the Omega Metroid with its Ice Beam. The Omega Metroid tore the copy down to its Core-X, and Samus absorbed it, regaining the Ice Beam. With it she destroyed the Omega Metroid; her gunship returned, flown by the Etecoons and Dachora she had freed, and she escaped moments before the station struck SR388.

Samus had killed four Omega Metroids before, on SR388 shortly before the Queen Metroid, during her extermination mission. At the BSL, the X copy that had hunted her across the station was what saved her.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Omega_Metroid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Restricted_Laboratory · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SA-X · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Docking_Bays · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion</sub>


### Fusion: Samus's defiance of the Federation at the BSL

*Keys:* defied the Federation, defy the Federation, defying the Federation, disobeyed orders, disobey orders, disobeying orders, Federation orders, Federation's orders, capture the SA-X, capture the X, withheld data, insubordination  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* BSL, B.S.L., SA-X, X Parasite, X Parasites, SR388, Adam, Biologic


During the BSL incident Samus Aran openly defied the Galactic Federation, destroying a Federation contractor's research station and a planet to do it (Metroid Fusion).

The truth came out in two parts. After the Restricted Laboratory was jettisoned, her ship computer admitted that the Federation had secretly bred Metroids aboard the Biologic Space Laboratories research station and that no fewer than ten SA-X were loose, and ordered her to leave. After she destroyed Neo-Ridley in SRX, it admitted the rest: the Federation had taken an interest in the X and meant to capture the SA-X for military purposes, and it had withheld her Plasma Beam data to keep her from engaging the SA-X, knowing she would try to destroy it. She was to leave the rest of the investigation to the Federation; the computer locked the Navigation Room's hatches to hold her.

Samus refused. She wanted the X destroyed before the Federation arrived and argued that the station had to be destroyed with the X inside, even if she died with it. The computer would not yield, for her own safety, until she addressed it as Adam and spoke of the real Adam Malkovich, the commander who had died saving her life. The computer confirmed it was him, his mind preserved by the Federation. He agreed the X were too dangerous and gave her the plan to drop the station onto SR388, ending his orders with his old "Any objections, Lady?" (see The Adam AI).

She carried it out and escaped before the Federation arrived, expecting to be held responsible (see The destruction of the BSL and SR388). Her standing survived: the Federation later sent her to ZDR when evidence surfaced that the X lived there, after its own seven E.M.M.I. had gone silent (Metroid Dread).

Samus had served in the Federation Army under Adam before going independent, and at the Bottle Ship she had already found Metroids bred in secret. At the BSL she found the Federation itself behind such a program, and put the galaxy's safety ahead of its orders.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Adam_Malkovich · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Operations_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/B.S.L_Research_Station · https://metroiddatabase.com/endings-metroid-fusion/</sub>


### Fusion: Sectors of the BSL station

*Keys:* SRX, TRO, PYR, AQA, NOC, Sector 1, Sector 2, Sector 3, Sector 4, Sector 5, Sector 6, BSL sectors  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* BSL, B.S.L., SA-X, X Parasite, X Parasites, Core-X, Biologic, Fusion Suit


The Biologic Space Laboratories research station held six sealed habitat sectors around its Main Deck, each reproducing an environment for breeding collected organisms. During the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion) Samus crossed them out of order; abilities she regained at each are noted in parentheses. Blue X, mutated by cold, drift between ARC and NOC; until she regained the Varia Suit, the vaccine's cold vulnerability kept her out of frigid areas.

- Sector 1 (SRX) reproduces SR388 (per the manual): rocky ground, lava and acid pools. X seized its five atmospheric stabilizers, which Samus cleared first (Charge Beam). Late in the mission Neo-Ridley, risen from Ridley's carcass in Sub-Zero Containment, attacked her here (Screw Attack).
- Sector 2 (TRO) is a humid tropical habitat of plant life, Zeros and Kihunters. Samus downloaded Bomb data here, then fought Zazabi (Hi-Jump Boots) and first saw the SA-X, which opened a hatch with its Ice Beam. Nettori, an X-grown plant wrapped around a Chozo Statue, spread vines through the station and cut its power; Samus returned, chased by the SA-X, to destroy it (Plasma Beam).
- Sector 3 (PYR) is superheated volcanic terrain. Samus downloaded Super Missile data here and was ambushed by the security robot B.O.X., which wrecked the Data Room and fled. Later the X shut down the cooling unit, and she had six minutes to reach the Main Boiler Control Room before the heat set off the station's self-destruct; an X in a crew member's shape met her there (Wide Beam).
- Sector 4 (AQA) is an aquatic habitat where X damage severed wiring and electrified much of the water. Samus destroyed the X-mimicked Serris (Speed Booster) and later, with the Gravity Suit, recovered Diffusion Missile data.
- Sector 5 (ARC) is an arctic habitat where the gravity weapon Nightmare was kept; its shadow passed over Samus on her first visit (Ice Missiles). She returned for Power Bomb data, briefly meeting the SA-X, and finally destroyed the X-infested Nightmare (Gravity Suit).
- Sector 6 (NOC) is a dark cave habitat for nocturnal creatures. Hidden in a vent as a Morph Ball, Samus watched the SA-X break a barrier with a Power Bomb. A Mega Core-X stole the Varia Suit data, which she reclaimed by destroying it. She later destroyed the X-infested B.O.X. here (Wave Beam). From NOC a lock that opens from one side leads into the Restricted Zone and the Restricted Laboratory.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_1_(SRX) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_2_(TRO) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_3_(PYR) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_4_(AQA) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_5_(ARC) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_6_(NOC)</sub>


### Fusion: The BSL incident (Metroid Fusion events)

*Keys:* BSL incident, B.S.L. incident, BSL mission, B.S.L. mission, Fusion incident, Metroid Fusion, SR388 survey, SR388 escort mission


The BSL incident was Samus Aran's mission aboard the Biologic Space Laboratories research station orbiting SR388, which ended with her dropping the station onto the planet (Metroid Fusion).

With SR388's Metroids exterminated, the Galactic Federation assigned Samus to escort a Biologic survey team studying the planet. There she killed a Hornoad; it dissolved into a formless X Parasite, which entered her and infested her central nervous system. She lost consciousness while piloting her gunship; an escape pod ejected her before the ship crashed into an asteroid belt. Surgeons cut away infected portions of her Power Suit but could not reach the X in her nerves. A vaccine made from cells of the baby Metroid she had once taken from SR388 saved her, leaving her with Metroid DNA, able to absorb X, vulnerable to cold, and clad in the Fusion Suit.

Then an explosion struck the station's Quarantine Bay, where the SR388 samples and her infected suit parts were stored. The Federation sent her back in a new gunship whose onboard computer issued her orders; she privately named it Adam after her late commander Adam Malkovich. She was to find and exterminate the X aboard, which had already copied crew and specimens across the station and its six habitat sectors (SRX, TRO, PYR, AQA, ARC, NOC).

Regaining abilities from Data Rooms and from the Core-X of copies she destroyed, Samus faced: an X-mimicked Arachnus on the Main Deck; X in the five atmospheric stabilizers of Sector 1 (SRX); Zazabi in Sector 2 (TRO), where she first saw the SA-X, an X in her own form, and was ordered to avoid it; Serris in Sector 4 (AQA); B.O.X. in Sector 3 (PYR), which fled; a Mega Core-X that stole the Varia Suit data in Sector 6 (NOC); a six-minute race to PYR's sabotaged Main Boiler, where an X in a crew member's shape proved X absorb their victims' knowledge; Yakuza in the Reactor Silo; Nettori, whose vines had cut the station's power, in TRO; Nightmare, a gravity weapon turned X host, in ARC; and the X-infested B.O.X. in NOC. Hunted by the SA-X, she reached the Restricted Laboratory, where an SA-X attacked the Federation's secret Metroids and the laboratory was jettisoned.

The computer then admitted the Metroid breeding program and that, since X reproduce asexually, no fewer than ten SA-X were aboard, and ordered her to leave. On her way a Core-X rose from Ridley's frozen carcass in Sub-Zero Containment and attacked her in SRX as Neo-Ridley; she destroyed it. Only then did the computer admit that the Federation meant to capture the SA-X and had withheld her Plasma Beam data to keep her from engaging it. Samus refused to stand down; the computer revealed itself as Adam and sided with her. She reduced an SA-X to a fleeing Core-X on the Operations Deck, set the station's orbit to intercept SR388, and in the Docking Bay survived an Omega Metroid when that SA-X returned in her form and attacked it; absorbing its Core-X restored her Ice Beam. Her gunship, flown by the Etecoons and Dachora she had freed from the Habitation Deck, carried her clear before the station struck SR388, destroying the planet and the X on both. Samus expected tribunals and investigations; Adam told her one of them would understand.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/B.S.L_Research_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SA-X · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quarantine_Bay · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Restricted_Laboratory</sub>


### Fusion: The destruction of the BSL and SR388

*Keys:* destruction of SR388, destroyed SR388, destroy SR388, destroying SR388, crashed into SR388, crash into SR388, SR388's destruction, SR388 destroyed, intercept SR388, destruction of the BSL, BSL's destruction, tribunals and investigations


The Biologic Space Laboratories research station and the planet SR388 were destroyed together at the end of the BSL incident, by Samus Aran's choice (Metroid Fusion). Once the Federation's intent to capture the SA-X was known, Samus insisted the X be destroyed before the Federation arrived, even if she died with the station. Her ship computer, which had just revealed itself to be Adam Malkovich, agreed and gave her the plan: alter the station's orbit so that it fell onto SR388, destroying the station, the planet and the X on both. "Samus, this is your last mission," he said, and closed: "Move quickly, and stay alive. That's an order! Any objections, Lady?"

Samus climbed to the Operations Deck. At the sealed hatch of the Operations Room an SA-X attacked her, matching her weapon for weapon; she wore it down with the Wave Beam until it swelled into a monstrous form and then collapsed into a bare Core-X that fled. Inside, she started the station's propulsion and adjusted its orbit path to intercept SR388. Three minutes remained. In the Docking Bay her gunship was missing and an Omega Metroid nearly killed her, until the same SA-X returned and attacked it and she absorbed the copy's Core-X to regain the Ice Beam (see Omega Metroid of the BSL). The gunship returned, she boarded, and it pulled clear seconds before the station struck SR388. The impact destroyed the planet, the station and the X on both.

The ship could only be started manually, yet it had moved. Aboard were the Etecoons and Dachora she had freed from the Habitation Deck, species she had first met on Zebes; when she wondered whether Adam was responsible, he answered only, "They lent me a hand." The Dachora was later seen with hatched young.

In her closing reflection Samus judged that her reunion with Adam may have saved the universe, a danger others, limited by their own experience, might never grasp. "They will hold tribunals and investigations. They will hold us responsible," she said. Adam replied: "Do not worry. One of them will understand. One of them must."

For Samus, SR388 joined Zebes, Dark Aether and Phaaze among the worlds destroyed in her wake, and it was the first she deliberately set on that course.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Operations_Deck · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SR388 · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SA-X · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Dachora · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Etecoon</sub>


### Fusion: The Restricted Laboratory and the secret Metroid program

*Keys:* Restricted Laboratory, Restricted Lab, Restricted Labs, peaceful application, peaceful applications, BSL Metroids, BSL Metroid program, Restricted Zone


The Restricted Laboratory was a high-security facility in the Restricted Zone of the Biologic Space Laboratories research station. The zone is mapped with the Main Deck but entered from Sector 6 (NOC) through a lock that opens from one side, and every hatch inside carried the station's highest security grade. There, in secret and under Galactic Federation authority, Metroids were bred and studied, a species believed extinct since Samus exterminated it on SR388. The laboratory held larval Metroids and Alpha, Gamma and Zeta forms.

The ship computer laid the program out: "the Federation has been secretly working on a Metroid breeding program. For peaceful application only, of course." It called SRX, the station's replica of SR388, ideal for raising Alpha, Gamma, Zeta and even Omega Metroids, and said the research had uncovered techniques for rapid growth, enough to create an Omega from a larval Metroid in days, before declaring "that research is finished." The X, it said, had "smelled out its natural enemy."

Samus entered near the end of the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion), hunted by the SA-X after destroying the X-infested B.O.X. in NOC. The Metroids left her alone. An SA-X broke in and attacked them; they swarmed and destroyed it, and the fighting set off fires that triggered the laboratory's safeguard, a sixty-second countdown to detachment. Samus escaped before the section was jettisoned and exploded, taking the Metroids with it.

The computer then admitted that, because X reproduce asexually, no fewer than ten SA-X were aboard, and ordered her to leave the station. This was the second concealed Metroid program Samus had uncovered, after the Bottle Ship incident. The discovery, together with the Federation's plan to capture the SA-X, drove her to defy its orders and destroy the station. A full-grown Omega Metroid later met her in the Docking Bay (see Omega Metroid of the BSL).


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Restricted_Laboratory · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Restricted_Zone · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SA-X · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_6_(NOC)</sub>


## Dread


### Dread: ADAM on ZDR and Raven Beak's impersonation
