*Keys:* Adam, fake Adam, false Adam, impersonated Adam, impersonating Adam, Adam impersonation, Adam's voice, Communication Room, Communication Rooms  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* ZDR, Raven Beak, Itorash, Quiet Robe, E.M.M.I., EMMI, Hanubia, Metroid Dread


On ZDR, Samus Aran's only link to the outside was ADAM, the computer of her Gunship, which carries the mind of her former commanding officer Adam Malkovich (Metroid Dread). Jamming signals across the planet block wireless communication, so she could reach ADAM only from dedicated communication rooms, where she reported in, received briefings on the planet and her enemies, and was told where to go next.

For much of the mission the voice answering her was not ADAM. Raven Beak, the Mawkin leader who had ambushed her on arrival, had been posing as ADAM in those communications. In that guise he directed her across the planet, and every hardship he steered her into served one purpose: to awaken the Metroid DNA in her body so that he could clone an army of Metroids from her. He revealed the deception himself aboard Itorash, before their final battle, and offered her a place beside him.

The real ADAM spoke at the end. When Samus, her Metroid nature fully awake, reached her Gunship as ZDR began to self-destruct, ADAM warned her not to take the controls: her power would drain the ship as it had drained Itorash. Only after the X that had taken Quiet Robe was absorbed into her and brought that power under control could she fly out.

ADAM is the voice Samus relies on in the field, the echo of a commander she respected, and on ZDR a Chozo who had stood at her adoption used that voice to lead her step by step toward the fate he had chosen for her. Adam Malkovich himself, and the ADAM of the BSL incident, have their own histories.


<sub>Sources: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adam_Malkovich · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/exploration/index.html</sub>


### Dread: Areas of ZDR

*Keys:* Artaria, Cataris, Dairon, Burenia, Ferenia, Ghavoran, Elun, Hanubia, E.M.M.I. Zone, E.M.M.I. Zones, EMMI Zone, EMMI Zones


ZDR's underground is divided into regions joined by elevators, shuttles and teleporters. Several hold E.M.M.I. Zones, sealed districts each patrolled by a single E.M.M.I. and run by a Central Unit. Samus Aran crossed all of them on her way from the depths to the surface (Metroid Dread).

- Artaria: the deep region where Raven Beak left Samus after defeating her, with one large super-heated zone and several smaller ones. Organic life survives here, including the creature Corpius. The damaged E.M.M.I.-01P and the white E.M.M.I.-02SM patrolled it; Samus recovered the Charge Beam, Spider Magnet, Varia Suit, Screw Attack and Grapple Beam here. Elevators lead to Cataris, Dairon and Burenia.
- Cataris: northeast of Artaria and east of Dairon; the lava region, home of the central system that circulates ZDR's magma to power its facilities, so its heat spreads into neighboring areas. Kraid was chained here, and the X mimic Experiment Z-57, escaped from Dairon, attacked the central system here. The green E.M.M.I.-03MB patrolled its zone; the Diffusion Beam was recovered here.
- Dairon: northwest of Cataris; an industrial region run from a generator and crowded with Chozo-made robots, with no major organic life. The yellow E.M.M.I.-04SB hunted here, and Samus recovered the Bomb.
- Burenia: a marine region, almost fully submerged and full of aquatic creatures found nowhere else, ruled by Drogyga. Shuttles run to Dairon, elevators to Artaria and Ghavoran, and teleporters to Ghavoran and Ferenia. The Flash Shift and Gravity Suit were recovered here.
- Ferenia: a ruined Chozo sanctuary where rituals were once held, almost entirely artificial, and the home of Quiet Robe. The purple E.M.M.I.-06WB and the X-mutated Quetzoa Escue were here; the Space Jump and Storm Missiles were recovered here.
- Ghavoran: an underground forest lit by sunlight from above, thick with Quetzoa and home to Golzuna, an X-mutated Muzby. The blue E.M.M.I.-05IM patrolled here; Super Missiles and Cross Bombs were found here.
- Elun: Raven Beak's quarantine facility, where the X-infected Mawkin were sealed. The Plasma Beam was kept here, and the opening of Elun loosed the X across ZDR.
- Hanubia: the surface region, with no native life of note. The red E.M.M.I.-07PB made its last stand here, and here Samus's Metroid power first emerged. Her Gunship waited on the surface.
- Itorash: Raven Beak's airship, stationed above Hanubia; it crashed to the surface after his defeat.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/E.M.M.I. · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Spider_Magnet · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Charge_Beam · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/report/vol8/index.html · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/exploration/index.html</sub>


### Dread: Chozo Soldiers of the Mawkin

*Keys:* Chozo Soldier, Chozo Soldiers, Robot Chozo Soldier, Robot Chozo Soldiers, Chozo Robot Soldier, Chozo Robot Soldiers, Mawkin soldier, Mawkin soldiers, Mawkin warrior, Mawkin warriors


The Chozo Soldiers of ZDR are the warriors of the Mawkin, the militant Chozo tribe led by Raven Beak. The Mawkin worked with the Thoha to contain the Metroids on SR388, followed Raven Beak to ZDR after he massacred the Thoha, and were meant to be the army with which he would conquer the galaxy. Instead, an X Parasite carried by one infected soldier spread through them. Raven Beak sealed the infected tribe inside the Elun facility, and when Elun was opened the X poured out across the planet (Metroid Dread).

Samus Aran fought the Mawkin's soldiers in two forms:
- X-infected Chozo Soldiers: Mawkin warriors whose bodies the X had taken. Every living Chozo Soldier Samus faced on ZDR was an X mimic, armed with the Mawkin's long spear-like weapon and fighting with a Chozo warrior's agility and strength.
- Robot Chozo Soldiers: combat machines Raven Beak built with Chozo technology once the X had taken his living troops. Their arms work as electromagnetic blades for high-speed charges, and their arm cannons fire orbs of light. They guard ZDR's facilities in his service. One of them killed Quiet Robe moments after he opened Samus's way forward.

Above them all stood Raven Beak himself, larger than any of his soldiers and the strongest warrior of the tribe.

The Mawkin are the Chozo at their most martial, the opposite of the Thoha scientists, and they share blood with Samus: Raven Beak's DNA was among that given to her as a child. By the time she left ZDR no Mawkin remained alive, and the Robot Chozo Soldiers were destroyed with the planet.


<sub>Sources: https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/report/vol7/index.html · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chozo · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Raven_Beak · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe</sub>


### Dread: E.M.M.I. (Extraplanetary Multiform Mobile Identifiers)

*Keys:* E.M.M.I., E.M.M.I, EMMI, EMMIs, E.M.M.I.s, Extraplanetary Multiform Mobile Identifier, Extraplanetary Multiform Mobile Identifiers, Federation research robots, E.M.M.I. robots, EMMI robots


E.M.M.I., Extraplanetary Multiform Mobile Identifiers, are survey robots of the Galactic Federation, built specifically to capture unknown organisms and extract their DNA. Seven formed a special unit sent to planet ZDR to investigate a transmission showing a living X Parasite. Contact with all seven was lost, and Samus Aran was sent after them (Metroid Dread).

An E.M.M.I. is a slim, humanoid machine with three-segmented limbs ending in four radially symmetric claws. Its front and back are nearly identical and its joints rotate both ways, so it reverses direction instantly and moves as easily on walls and ceilings as on floors. Armor plating, colored differently on each unit, covers the face, upper torso, forearms and upper legs. Its body is made of a special material said to be the hardest in the universe; Samus's beams, missiles and bombs do nothing to it. It hunts by sound, closing straight in on any noise in range, and once its visual sensors catch its target it pursues without hesitation.

Each unit keeps to its own E.M.M.I. Zone. The gates between a zone and the rest of the facility let only Samus through, and they close temporarily while an E.M.M.I. is chasing her, so she must shake it off before she can leave. The Phantom Cloak hides her from their sensors and silences her footsteps.

When an E.M.M.I. seizes Samus it pins her and begins extracting her DNA, which kills her within moments; she has two fleeting chances to break the hold with a Melee Counter. The only weapon that harms one is the Omega Cannon, which her Arm Cannon becomes after she absorbs the energy of a zone's Central Unit.

On ZDR the E.M.M.I. no longer served the Federation. Raven Beak, leader of the Mawkin, reprogrammed them to kill Samus on sight and harvest the Metroid DNA in her body so that he could clone an army of Metroids. After the X were loosed from Elun, an X wearing Quiet Robe's body reactivated the units he had shut down. Samus destroyed all seven; the last, in Hanubia, fell to her awakening Metroid power, which drained it as it tried to drain her. To Samus the E.M.M.I. were the dread of ZDR: relentless, nearly untouchable, and built by the people who sent her.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/E.M.M.I. · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/report/vol1/index.html · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/report/vol2/index.html · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/dread/index.html · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Raven_Beak</sub>


### Dread: Great creatures of ZDR

*Keys:* Corpius, Drogyga, Escue, Golzuna, Experiment Z-57, Experiment No. Z-57, Z-57, Quetzoa, Muzby, Kraid on ZDR


Besides the E.M.M.I. and Raven Beak's soldiers, several great creatures barred Samus Aran's way on ZDR, three of them X mimics (Metroid Dread).

- Corpius: the first of ZDR's great creatures Samus faced after Raven Beak left her in Artaria. A giant, skull-faced creature that drives its tail into the ground, sweeps it across its lair and spits lumps of acid; Samus could slide beneath it.
- Kraid: the second, and not native to ZDR. The monster of Zebes, whom Samus had already beaten twice there, survived that planet's fall, was captured and experimented on by Raven Beak, and was chained by wall and ceiling in the magma of Cataris, his belly pocked with purple blemishes and gaping holes where his spikes had been. Samus killed him once more; an X later took his corpse, and it was this X that infected Raven Beak at the end.
- Drogyga: the master of Burenia's flooded depths, an aquatic creature that Samus fought in the submerged ruins there.
- Escue: a Quetzoa, one of the winged predators of Ferenia and Ghavoran, mutated by the X into a far larger and deadlier form in Ferenia. Destroyed, it collapsed into a Core-X whose absorption gave Samus the Storm Missiles.
- Golzuna: a Muzby of Ghavoran's jungle, transformed by the X. Its Core-X yielded the Cross Bomb.
- Experiment Z-57, also recorded as Experiment No. Z-57: a creature Samus had passed under study in Dairon; it escaped and, likewise an X mimic, attacked the central system in Cataris that heats the planet.

The X-mutated creatures were not animals Samus simply hunted; they were the X itself wearing the forms of ZDR's fauna, and each one she killed she absorbed, as only she can. With ZDR's destruction, every one of these creatures is gone.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Kraid · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/report/vol11/index.html · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kraid · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread</sub>


### Dread: Itorash and the end of ZDR

*Keys:* Itorash, destruction of ZDR, ZDR's destruction, end of ZDR, ZDR exploded, ZDR explosion, escape from ZDR, final battle on ZDR, Raven Beak's ship, Raven Beak's airship


Itorash was Raven Beak's airship, a floating fortress he built and kept stationed in the sky above Hanubia, ZDR's surface region. It was the seat of his power and the place where Samus Aran's mission on ZDR reached its climax (Metroid Dread).

Samus reached Itorash after destroying the last E.M.M.I. on the surface. There Raven Beak revealed that he had been posing as ADAM, the ship's computer whose voice she had been following from the communication rooms. He had spared her life in Artaria and driven her through every hardship since so that the Metroid DNA in her body would awaken; he meant to clone her into an army of Metroids and rule the galaxy with it. He offered her a place beside him. She refused, and they fought.

Raven Beak brought her to the edge of death. What saved her was the thing he had been cultivating: her Metroid nature surged, her suit took on the form known as the Metroid Suit, and she drained the energy from him as a Metroid drains its prey. Breaking free, she drained Itorash itself; the fortress lost power and crashed onto the surface of ZDR. There Raven Beak, broken, was infected by an X that had possessed Kraid's corpse, and Samus destroyed the Raven Beak X with the Hyper Beam her transformed Arm Cannon now fired.

The destruction of the Raven Beak X set off ZDR's self-destruct. Samus reached her Gunship, where the real ADAM warned her not to take the controls: in her state she would drain the ship as she had drained Itorash. The X that had taken Quiet Robe's body then appeared behind her. She raised her Arm Cannon; it bowed. She lowered the weapon, and the X returned to its base form and was absorbed into her, letting her control the ship without draining it. She lifted off moments before ZDR exploded, a blast presumed to have destroyed the last of the X.

ZDR no longer exists, and Itorash was lost with it. Samus left carrying a Metroid's power and the remains of the X that keeps it in check.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://nc.inverse.com/gaming/metroid-dread-ending-explained-metroid-suit-rewards</sub>


### Dread: Planet ZDR

*Keys:* ZDR, planet ZDR, ZDR's surface, depths of ZDR, Chozo facilities of ZDR


ZDR is a remote planet, unexplored by humanity, and the place where Samus Aran fought the Chozo warlord Raven Beak (Metroid Dread). It no longer exists: it tore itself apart moments after Samus lifted off in her Gunship.

Its temperature, humidity, atmosphere and gravity closely resemble those of SR388. What mattered lay underground: a maze of Chozo-built facilities, still full of Chozo machines, running from the surface down through layer after layer of crust and divided into regions linked by elevators and shuttles: Artaria, Cataris, Dairon, Burenia, Ferenia, Ghavoran, Elun and Hanubia, with Raven Beak's airship Itorash hanging above the surface. A central system in the lava region of Cataris circulates the planet's magma to generate energy for the facilities, which is why super-heated zones in Artaria and Cataris are lethal without the Varia Suit. Jamming signals block wireless contact, so communication with a ship's computer is possible only from dedicated communication rooms. Native ZDR wildlife is ferocious.

ZDR became the refuge of the Mawkin, the warrior tribe of the Chozo. Raven Beak brought his forces there, with the captive Thoha scientist Quiet Robe, after massacring the Thoha. An X Parasite carried by an infected soldier spread through the Mawkin; Raven Beak sealed the infected tribe in the Elun facility and replaced his warriors with Robot Chozo Soldiers. By the time Samus arrived, he and Quiet Robe were the only living Chozo on the planet, and Kraid, captured and experimented on by Raven Beak, hung in chains in Cataris.

The Galactic Federation learned of ZDR when footage of a living X reached it, footage Raven Beak had allowed to leak. It sent seven E.M.M.I. research robots; when contact with them was lost, it dispatched Samus, the only being immune to the X. Raven Beak ambushed her on arrival, stripped most of her suit's abilities and left her in the depths, and she fought her way back to the surface. Once the X were loosed from Elun they overran the planet's ecosystem, and the explosion that destroyed ZDR is presumed to have wiped out the last of the X. For Samus, ZDR is where her Metroid DNA awakened and where Quiet Robe died.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/report/vol8/index.html · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/exploration/index.html · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/dread/index.html · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters</sub>


### Dread: Quiet Robe

*Keys:* Quiet Robe, Quiet-Robe, Quietrobe, Quiet Robe X, Thoha scientist, last Thoha, last of the Thoha


Quiet Robe was a male Chozo scientist of the Thoha tribe and the last of his people. The Thoha engineered the Metroids on SR388 to combat the X; when his tribe resolved to destroy the planet and the Metroids with it, the Mawkin leader Raven Beak slaughtered them, sparing Quiet Robe alone because only the Thoha could control Metroids. Raven Beak carried him to ZDR as a hostage, the key to the Metroid army he meant to raise. After the X infected the rest of the Mawkin, Quiet Robe and Raven Beak were the only living Chozo on the planet; Quiet Robe dwelt in Ferenia, a ruined sanctuary where the Chozo once held rituals (Metroid Dread).

He met Samus Aran there. The purple E.M.M.I., 06WB, had seized her, and Quiet Robe shut it down before it could kill her. He introduced himself and told her his story and Raven Beak's: the massacre of the Thoha, Raven Beak's plan to make the Metroids his weapons, and his new plan to extract the Metroid DNA in Samus's body through the reprogrammed E.M.M.I. and clone an army from it. Samus told him she would finish the fight. He opened a barrier so she could press on, and moments later one of Raven Beak's Robot Chozo Soldiers killed him.

His death did not end his part in the mission. When the X were loosed from Elun, one took his corpse; the Quiet Robe X re-enabled the E.M.M.I. he had disabled and roamed ZDR. Yet after Samus destroyed Raven Beak and reached her Gunship, where ADAM warned that her awakened Metroid power would drain the ship if she took the controls, the Quiet Robe X appeared behind her. She leveled her Arm Cannon; it bowed. She lowered her weapon, and the X returned to its base form and was absorbed into her. With it inside her, her Metroid power was brought under control and she could pilot the ship off the exploding planet.

Quiet Robe is the only Chozo of ZDR who helped Samus, and what remains of him travels with her still.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/E.M.M.I. · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/report/vol8/index.html · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters</sub>


### Dread: Raven Beak

*Keys:* Raven Beak, Ravenbeak, Raven-Beak, Raven Beak X, Mawkin leader, leader of the Mawkin, Hadar sen olmen


Raven Beak is a male Chozo, leader of the Mawkin warrior tribe, and the enemy Samus Aran faced on ZDR (Metroid Dread). His creed is "Hadar... sen olmen": power is everything. He is markedly larger than Samus and than his own soldiers, and wears on his right arm a cannon like hers. He first appeared in the Chozo Memories of SR388, as the Chozo leader standing over the body of another leader while overlooking his army (Metroid: Samus Returns).

Raven Beak was present when the Chozo adopted the orphaned Samus, and some of his DNA was given to her. On SR388 the Mawkin worked with the Thoha scientists to contain the Metroids the Thoha had engineered. When the Thoha resolved to destroy the planet, Metroids and all, Raven Beak, who coveted the Metroids as weapons for galactic conquest, massacred the Thoha, sparing only Quiet Robe because only the Thoha could control Metroids. He withdrew with his forces and his hostage to ZDR to raise Metroids there. An infected soldier brought the X among the Mawkin; Raven Beak locked the infected tribe in Elun and built Robot Chozo Soldiers to replace them, leaving himself and Quiet Robe the only living Chozo on the planet. He also captured Kraid and experimented on him.

Samus's extermination of the Metroids on SR388 changed his plan rather than ending it: the Metroid DNA in her body would be his breeding stock. He let footage of a living X reach the Galactic Federation, reprogrammed the seven E.M.M.I. it sent to kill her and harvest her DNA, ambushed her on arrival, destroyed the exit, stripped her suit of most of its abilities and left her alive in the depths. Posing as her ship's computer ADAM, he steered her through ordeal after ordeal so that her Metroid nature would awaken. Aboard his airship Itorash he revealed himself, told her he had spared her for exactly this, and offered her a place beside him ruling the galaxy with an army of Metroids cloned from her. She refused and fought.

He nearly killed her. Her Metroid power saved her: she drained him, then drained Itorash itself, which crashed onto ZDR. On the surface an X that had possessed Kraid's corpse infected the broken Chozo, and Samus destroyed the Raven Beak X with her new power. To Samus he is the Chozo who stood at her adoption and gave her part of his DNA, and who came to see her only as a vessel for the Metroid DNA he wanted.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Raven_Beak · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/report/vol7/index.html · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid:_Samus_Returns · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chozo</sub>


### Dread: The Chozo tribes - Thoha, Mawkin and Raven Beak's coup

*Keys:* Thoha, Mawkin, Thoha massacre, Mawkin tribe, Thoha tribe, Chozo of ZDR, Mawkin Chozo, Thoha Chozo, Chozo tribes  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* ZDR, Raven Beak, Quiet Robe, Elun, massacre, coup, SR388, tribe, Metroid Dread


Two Chozo tribes shaped the history that ended on ZDR: the Thoha, peaceful scientists, and the Mawkin, a warrior tribe led by Raven Beak (Metroid Dread; Metroid: Samus Returns).

The Thoha came to SR388 for research and there met the X Parasite, which uses the planet's creatures as hosts. To combat it they engineered the Metroids. The X were suppressed and the Chozo lived in peace for a time, but the Metroids grew out of control and came to threaten the Chozo civilization itself. The Mawkin worked with the Thoha to contain them.

The alliance broke when the Thoha decided to destroy SR388 outright to end the Metroid threat. Raven Beak, who coveted the Metroids as weapons for galactic conquest, turned on his allies and killed the Thoha to the last, except for the scientist Quiet Robe, spared because only the Thoha could control Metroids. The Chozo Memories of SR388 preserve the moment: one Chozo leader standing over the body of the other, overlooking his army.

Raven Beak took his warriors and his hostage to ZDR to raise Metroids there. The X followed: one infected soldier spread the parasite through the Mawkin until only Raven Beak and Quiet Robe were untouched. Raven Beak sealed the infected tribe in the Elun facility and built Robot Chozo Soldiers to replace them. There the X stayed until Samus Aran's climb toward the surface took her through Elun and they were loosed across the planet.

Samus, raised by the Chozo on Zebes, shares blood with the Mawkin: Raven Beak was present at her adoption and was among the Chozo who gave her their DNA. She learned this history from Quiet Robe in Ferenia. The Thoha ended when Quiet Robe died helping her; the Mawkin ended with Raven Beak, who died trying to use her Metroid DNA. By the time ZDR exploded, both tribes were gone.


<sub>Sources: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid:_Samus_Returns · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chozo · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Metroid_characters · https://www.nintendo.com/jp/switch/ayl8a/report/vol7/index.html · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Raven_Beak · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe</sub>


### Dread: The seven E.M.M.I. units and their Central Units

*Keys:* Central Unit, Central Units, E.M.M.I. units, EMMI units, 01P, 02SM, 03MB, 04SB, 05IM, 06WB, 07PB, 07B


Seven E.M.M.I. were deployed to ZDR, each identified by a number and a letter code. Six of them carried a Federation ability module named by that code, and destroying such a unit let Samus Aran take its ability for herself (Metroid Dread). Each unit patrolled its own E.M.M.I. Zone, and each zone was run by a Central Unit, that district's mother computer.

- E.M.M.I.-01P: met in Artaria soon after Raven Beak's ambush. Already heavily damaged, missing armor plates and a claw, unable to climb walls or ceilings, and carrying no module. In the next chamber Samus found its Central Unit already destroyed, absorbed its energy, and shot 01P with her first Omega Cannon as it came through the door.
- E.M.M.I.-02SM: white, in Artaria. Climbed walls and ceilings but could not enter passages small enough for the Morph Ball. Samus melted its faceplate with the Omega Stream and finished it with the Omega Blaster. Yielded the Spider Magnet.
- E.M.M.I.-03MB: green, in Cataris. Like 02SM but able to squeeze through tight spaces. Yielded the Morph Ball.
- E.M.M.I.-04SB: yellow, in Dairon. Faster than the earlier units but still outrunnable; Samus fetched the Flash Shift from Burenia to evade it. Yielded the Speed Booster and Shinespark.
- E.M.M.I.-05IM: blue, in Ghavoran. Its long-range vision froze Samus where she stood, but it could not close the zone gates behind her. Yielded Ice Missiles.
- E.M.M.I.-06WB: purple, in Ferenia. Saw through walls and fired energy orbs that stunned its prey. It seized Samus until Quiet Robe shut it down; the X that later took his body switched it back on. Yielded the Wave Beam.
- E.M.M.I.-07PB: red, in Hanubia. Its explosive blasts stunned Samus. It pinned her and began extracting her Metroid DNA, and she drained its energy with her bare hand, the first use of her Metroid power. The drain destroyed its Central Unit as well, so no Omega Cannon was needed. Yielded the Power Bomb.

A Central Unit is a stationary brain. Its armor, once broken, exposes an eye and a mass of brain tissue. When Samus destroys one it drops from its mount and she absorbs its energy, turning her Arm Cannon into the Omega Cannon until that zone's E.M.M.I. is dead. The Omega Stream burns through an E.M.M.I.'s face protector and the Omega Blaster destroys the core beneath, after which the cannon fades.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/E.M.M.I. · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Central_Unit · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Omega_Cannon_(Metroid_Dread) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Spider_Magnet · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Speed_Booster · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Power_Bomb</sub>


### Dread: The ZDR mission

*Keys:* Metroid Dread, mission on ZDR, mission to ZDR, ZDR incident, ZDR mission, events on ZDR, what happened on ZDR, Dread mission


Samus Aran's mission to ZDR (Metroid Dread) began when the Galactic Federation received a transmission showing a living X Parasite, a species presumed to have died with SR388. It sent seven E.M.M.I. research robots to the remote planet; when contact was lost, it sent Samus, the only being in the galaxy immune to the X.

She landed in her Gunship and at once met a living Chozo warrior with an arm cannon like her own. He destroyed the exit, defeated her outright, stripped most of her suit's abilities and left her alive deep underground in Artaria. ADAM, her ship's computer, reachable only from dedicated communication rooms, told her to find another route to the surface. The reprogrammed E.M.M.I. hunted her through their zones; she turned her Arm Cannon into the Omega Cannon with the energy of their Central Units and destroyed them one by one, taking back her abilities as she went. In Cataris she killed Kraid, chained there by her enemy.

In Ferenia the Thoha scientist Quiet Robe saved her from the purple E.M.M.I. and told her the truth. Her attacker was Raven Beak, leader of the Mawkin, who had slaughtered the Thoha to seize the Metroids as weapons and, after Samus exterminated the Metroids on SR388, lured her to ZDR so the E.M.M.I. could harvest the Metroid DNA in her body and clone an army. Quiet Robe opened her path and was killed by a Robot Chozo Soldier. ADAM encouraged her to defeat Raven Beak and destroy ZDR.

In Elun the X that Raven Beak had quarantined got loose and overran the planet; one took Quiet Robe's corpse and reactivated the remaining E.M.M.I. In Hanubia the last E.M.M.I. pinned her, and she killed it by draining its energy with her hand. Her Metroid DNA had awakened, and with it she was slowly becoming a Metroid.

Aboard the airship Itorash, Raven Beak revealed he had been posing as ADAM and had spared her so her Metroid power would awaken. She refused to join him, drained him and Itorash, and brought the fortress down onto ZDR, where an X from Kraid's corpse took Raven Beak and she destroyed the Raven Beak X. As the planet began to self-destruct, the X that had taken Quiet Robe was absorbed into her at her Gunship, bringing her Metroid power under control, and she escaped before ZDR exploded, taking the last of the X with it.


<sub>Sources: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Dread · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/E.M.M.I. · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Quiet_Robe · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship</sub>


## Technology


### Technology: Bounty hunting in the Metroid galaxy

*Keys:* bounty hunter, bounty hunters, bounty hunting, bounty-hunting, space hunter, space hunters, hired gun, hired guns


Bounty hunters in Samus Aran's galaxy are space-faring mercenaries, paid for captures and for firepower alike. Per the original manual, when the Space Pirates became a threat the Federation Bureau and the Federation Police called together warriors known for their great courage, the space hunters, who received large rewards when they captured pirates; it named Samus the greatest of all the space hunters. The Galactic Federation and the Space Pirates are among the hunters' most profitable employers.

After leaving Federation service Samus worked freelance, most often for the Federation itself. It hired her to destroy Mother Brain and the Metroids on Zebes, to investigate the Alimbic message and secure its promised power for the Federation or keep it from others, to find the missing G.F.S. Tyr at Aether, to join the G.F.S. Olympus during the Phazon crisis, to scout the Space Pirates of the Bermuda System, to eradicate the Metroids of SR388, to stop the Pirates at Tanamaar, to protect Biologic Space Labs researchers on SR388, and to investigate the lost E.M.M.I. on ZDR. Several missions began on her own initiative: the Frigate Orpheon distress call, her pursuit of Ridley from Ceres to Zebes, and the Bottle Ship's Baby's Cry. Space Pirate records call her simply "the Hunter"; Gandrayda called her "Sammy".

Other hunters she has met:
- Rundas, Ghor and Gandrayda: hired with her by Fleet Admiral Castor Dane to purge a Space Pirate virus from the Federation's Aurora Units. All three were corrupted by Phazon, turned to command Space Pirate forces and destroyed by Samus. Rundas was an ice-shaping Phrygisian; Ghor a cyborg veteran of the war to liberate Wotan VII; Gandrayda a shape-shifter.
- Sylux: from Cylosis, flying the Delano 7. Once a Galactic Federation field captain, he disobeyed an order to hold position and wait for Samus, chased a Space Pirate superweapon, and was the only survivor of his platoon; Samus destroyed the weapon easily. Blaming her and the Federation, he deserted, shadowed her ship after the Phazon crisis, and led the Pirates at Tanamaar and on Viewros.
- Weavel, a Space Pirate warrior rebuilt as a cyborg after Samus nearly killed him; Kanden, an Enoema soldier who escaped the laboratory that made him; Trace, a Kriken; Noxus, a Vhozon who enforces his own justice; Spire, thought to be the last of the Diamonts. All raced her for the Alimbic message in the Tetra Galaxy.


<sub>Sources: https://metroiddatabase.com/old_site/m1/manual.php · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bounty_Hunter · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime_Hunters · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Weavel · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Kanden</sub>


### Technology: Elevators, transports and teleportals

*Keys:* Master Teleporter, Light Portal, Light Portals, Dark Portal, Dark Portals, teleportal, teleportals, Portal Generator, Portal Generators, Luminoth Elevator, Chozo elevator, Chozo elevators


Much of the transport on the worlds Samus explores was built by the Chozo, Luminoth and Lamorn, and still works long after its builders fell silent.

- Chozo systems: elevators link the regions of Zebes. On Tallon IV transport rooms such as Transport to Chozo Ruins West, Transport to Magmoor Caverns North and Transport to Phendrana Drifts South join the regions. ZDR's Chozo complexes use elevators between Artaria, Cataris, Dairon and Burenia and between Burenia and Ghavoran, two shuttles linking Burenia and Dairon above and below the water, and a teleportal joining Ghavoran and Ferenia.
- Luminoth systems on Aether: the Luminoth Elevator, the Light-Based Teleportation System, Lift Crystals and Liftvine Crystals, the Conveyor System and the Luminoth Supply Transport.
- Portals between Aether and Dark Aether: natural rifts torn open when a Leviathan's impact split Aether in two, which the Ing used to invade. Light Portals (white, on Dark Aether) lead back and Dark Portals (black, on Aether) lead across; each stays inert until energized by Light Beam or Dark Beam energy, some run one way only, and some vanish forever after one use. Luminoth portal generators in the Agon Wastes, Torvus Bog and Sanctuary Fortress, the Agon one focusing sunlight through industrial lenses, have safety systems that refuse to open onto an obstructed threshold. Space Pirates used the Dark Beam to energize a portal and send expeditions to Dark Aether. As Dark Aether collapsed after the Emperor Ing's death, Samus escaped through a portal.
- Federation installations: the BSL station's Main Elevator to the access shaft was wrecked by an unknown agent. When the Reactor Core shut down, the station's elevators and hatches failed; auxiliary power was not enough, and full power returned only after Samus destroyed the source of the roots choking the core. The Bottle Ship's Main Elevator connects the Main Sector to every sector except Sector Zero.
- Space Pirate transporter: on the Pirate Homeworld Samus reached a transporter to board a Leviathan Battleship so the Federation could open a wormhole to Phaaze.
- Lamorn transport: an ancient artifact unearthed at a Federation facility on Tanamaar, damaged during Samus's fight with Sylux, teleported everything nearby to Viewros. Her only way home was the Lamorn's Master Teleporter atop the Chrono Tower, which needed five keys scattered across the planet. Sylux badly damaged it, and Federation soldiers held him down while she escaped through it.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Tallon_Overworld · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/ZDR · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Luminoth_Technology · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Portal · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Aether · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion</sub>


### Technology: Navigation Rooms, Data Rooms and Network Stations

*Keys:* Navigation Room, Navigation Rooms, Navigation Booth, Navigation Booths, Network Station, Network Stations, Data Room, Data Rooms, Communication Room, Communication Rooms, ADAM computer


Navigation Rooms are the communication chambers of the Biologic Space Labs research station orbiting SR388; most stand at the entrances to its six sectors. Standing at the center, Samus uplinked to her gunship's onboard computer, which relayed the Galactic Federation's orders. Checking in was mandatory before she could proceed, and the computer controlled the rooms' devices and could seal her inside if she refused to cooperate. The rooms also hold sector maps she could download, and her gunship provides the same uplink. Samus named the computer Adam, after her former commanding officer. When she resolved to destroy the station rather than let the Federation capture the SA-X and the X for study, the computer at first opposed her, then revealed itself as the mind of Adam Malkovich and backed her plan, ending his orders with his old sign-off, "Any objections, Lady?"

Data Rooms on the same station let the Federation upload equipment data to her Power Suit, restoring systems removed during the surgery that saved her life. The X fought back: the computer warned that they had destroyed every Data Room she had already used, the SA-X blocked the Sector 2 room and forced her to Sector 3 for the Super Missile data, and the Mega Core-X absorbed the Varia Suit data for itself.

Aboard the Bottle Ship, Navigation Booths served as rest points where Samus recorded her status and restored her energy. They also linked her and the platoon to Adam Malkovich, who directed them from the Control Room and authorized her weapons as needed.

On ZDR, Network Stations are communication rooms scattered through the Chozo facilities. ADAM briefed Samus before she landed, but after she fell to Raven Beak on the surface, the voice guiding her at the Network Stations was Raven Beak masquerading as ADAM to push her toward awakening her Metroid DNA, a deception exposed on Itorash. The real ADAM spoke again only from her gunship, warning her that her awakened Metroid powers would drain it.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Navigation_Room · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Navigation_Booth · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Data_Room · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Adam_Malkovich · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bottle_Ship</sub>


### Technology: Planets and places of the galaxy (index)

*Keys:* FS-176, Bilium, Oormine II, Twin Tabula, Tetra Galaxy, Bermuda System, Phrygis, Bes III, Vho, Cylosis, Mondreus, Wotan VII


A reference to the worlds, systems and stations of Samus Aran's galaxy.

- FS-176 system, closest to farthest: Bilium, a small quarantined world home to the sentient virus Miteralis; Oormine II, a lifeless wasteland of nuclear dust storms; Twin Tabula, known for Twin Fever; Zebes, the Chozo world where Samus was raised, later Mother Brain's stronghold, now destroyed; Tallon IV, a Chozo colony poisoned by a Leviathan and mined by Space Pirates.
- SR388: Chozo-colonized homeworld of the Metroids and the X Parasites, destroyed when Samus crashed the BSL station into it.
- K-2L (per the manga): an Earth colony led by Rodney Aran that mined afloraltite; Ridley's raid wiped out its colonists, leaving Samus the sole survivor.
- Kalandor Region, in the Federation Solar System: Norion, a military base terraformed by Aurora Unit 486; Bryyo, the largest source of Fuel Gel and home of the Reptilicus; Elysia, a former Chozo outpost whose floating SkyTown houses the Elysians.
- Dasha Region, on the outskirts of Federation territory: Aether, rogue world of the Luminoth, split into Dark Aether by a Leviathan.
- Pirate Homeworld: acid-rained Space Pirate stronghold, never confirmed as their origin. Phaaze: the living Phazon world that spawned the Leviathans.
- Tetra Galaxy, beyond Federation reach, holding the Alimbic Cluster: the planets Alinos and Arcterra, the Celestial Archives, Vesper Defense Outpost and Oubliette.
- Hunter homeworlds: Phrygis, ice-mining moon of the little-known Bes III (Rundas); Vho, frigid rim world of the Vhozon (Noxus); Mondreus, world of the Diamonts (Spire); Cylosis, Sylux's listed place of origin; Wotan VII, where Ghor lost most of his body in a war of liberation.
- Bermuda System: Excelcion, Bion and Talvania, where the Federation Force fought the Space Pirates.
- Tanamaar, in the Desolan System: site of a Federation facility whose unearthed artifact sent Samus to Viewros, the Lamorn world of Sol Valley, the Fury Green jungle and the Chrono Tower.
- ZDR: a planet of Chozo-built complexes where Raven Beak's Mawkin based themselves, with regions such as Artaria, Cataris, Burenia and Elun and the airship Itorash; destroyed.
- Stations and ships: Ceres Space Colony, where Ridley seized the baby Metroid; Frigate Orpheon above Tallon IV; the Biologic Space Labs research station orbiting SR388; the Bottle Ship; the G.F.S. Olympus, Valhalla and Tyr.

Only K-2L and Zebes were ever Samus's home; Ridley's raid ended the first, and the second exploded as she escaped.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/FS-176 · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bilium · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Oormine_II · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Twin_Tabula · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Zebes · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Tallon_IV</sub>


### Technology: Samus's Gunship

*Keys:* gunship, gunships, Samus's ship, Samus' ship, Hunter-class, Command Visor, Landing Site, Landing Sites, Ship Missile, Ship Missiles, Ship Grapple, Landing Beacon


Samus Aran travels, rests and resupplies aboard a single-pilot gunship; per the official Metroid Prime website it is the only space-faring vessel ever registered under her name. Every ship she has flown is Hunter-class: heavily armed craft built as movable bases for one-person operations, with mobile energy-recharge systems, ammunition microfactories and an onboard computer that stores her mission data. She lands at Landing Sites and records her mission logs aboard. Most of her ships restore her suit's energy and ammunition when she boards, though the one she flew to the Bottle Ship could not.

Her ships:
- A red ship that carried her to Zebes against Mother Brain. Space Pirate fighters shot it down as she left, stranding her in her Zero Suit until she escaped in a stolen Pirate craft.
- The first Hunter-class gunship, her ship on Tallon IV and in the Alimbic Cluster. Per the official website it weighs 136 tons and mounts twin Power Beam turrets and a retractable rear Plasma Beam cannon.
- The classic ship. Aether's atmosphere damaged it on landing and it repaired itself at the Temple Grounds; after the Phazon crisis it carried her to SR388, out of Zebes before the planet exploded, and to the Bottle Ship.
- The Phazon-crisis ship, built to her own design at the Federation shipyard of Aliehs III, with a Chozo biotech computer of some artificial intelligence, self-repair, a hyperdrive, a Biohazard Scanner for Phazon and an advanced medical bay. It answers her Command Visor, which stayed offline until Rundas rescued her from Space Pirate ATCs on Norion; through it she called the ship to Landing Beacons, fired Ship Missiles against Maldium, and used the Ship Grapple to gather the parts of the Theronian Bomb that broke the Leviathan's shield on Elysia. It flew her from Norion to Bryyo, Elysia, the Pirate Homeworld and Phaaze, and Sylux's Delano 7 later tailed it.
- The violet ship. When an X Parasite infection made her black out, the classic ship drifted into an asteroid belt and ejected her in an escape pod. The Federation replaced it with one whose computer issued her orders, obedience being the condition of the gift; that computer proved to be Adam Malkovich. It later landed her on ZDR, and she flew it out as the planet exploded once absorbing the X that mimicked Quiet Robe stopped her Metroid powers from draining it.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gunship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Samus_Aran%27s_Gunship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Spacecraft_Classifications · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Command_Visor · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ship_Missile · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Theronian_Bomb</sub>


### Technology: Save, Recharge and Map Stations

*Keys:* Save Station, Save Stations, Save Room, Recharge Station, Recharge Stations, Recharge Room, Recharge Rooms, Map Station, Map Stations, Missile Station, Ammo Station, Missile Battery


Across the ruins, stations and vessels Samus explores, a family of recurring consoles sustains her Power Suit. They stand in Chozo and Luminoth ruins, aboard Space Pirate craft such as Frigate Orpheon and the Space Pirate Mother Ship, and in Galactic Federation installations such as the G.F.S. Olympus and the Biologic Space Labs research station.

- Save Stations: chambers where Samus pauses to record her mission logs and suit data. They are called Save Rooms on the BSL station and Navigation Booths aboard the Bottle Ship. Those of SR388, Zebes and the BSL station only record; those of Tallon IV, Aether, the Phazon crisis and the Bottle Ship also fully restore her energy, and the Space Pirate Mother Ship's stations restock her ammunition as well. Her gunship usually performs the same service, though not during the Bottle Ship incident.
- Missile Stations and Ammo Stations: Missile Stations on Tallon IV, such as the one in the Hall of the Elders in the Chozo Ruins, reload her Missile Launcher. Ammo Stations on Dark Aether, in places such as Ing Cache 3 of the Dark Agon Wastes, Dark Torvus Bog and the Ing Hive, refill her Missiles and her Dark, Light and Annihilator Beam ammunition.
- Recharge Rooms: throughout the BSL station, consoles into which Samus inserts her Arm Cannon fully restore her shielding and ammunition by an unexplained method. On SR388 during the Metroid extermination, the Missile Battery and Big Energy Ball aboard her gunship did the same.
- Map Stations: holographic consoles that download the surrounding region's layout into her suit. The Chozo maps of Zebes often omit whole sections and tunnels, and during the Phazon crisis Map Station data was often needed to find Landing Sites for her gunship.
- ZDR: the Chozo facilities hold Save Stations, separate energy and ammunition recharge stations, and Total Recharge Stations, Chozo idols traced with glowing golden lines that restore her energy, ammunition and Aeion reserves at once.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Save_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Map_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ammo_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Missile_Station · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Recharge_Room · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Navigation_Booth</sub>


### Technology: Species of the galaxy (index)

*Keys:* Kriken, Vhozon, Diamont, Diamonts, Enoema, Phrygisian, Phrygisians, Lamorn, grievers, Bryyonian, Bryyonians, Reptilicus


A reference to the sapient peoples of Samus Aran's galaxy; each line points to the entry that covers it in depth.

- Humans: the core of the Galactic Federation's military and research arms. Per the manga, Samus was born on K-2L, an Earth colony; Adam Malkovich and Anthony Higgs are human. See Galactic Federation.
- Chozo: the ancient bird-like civilization that raised Samus, infused her with Chozo blood and built her Power Suit; its tribes include the Thoha and the Mawkin. See Chozo.
- Space Pirates: the galaxy's raiders; the Zebesians are Pirates who seized Zebes and took its name. See Space Pirates and Zebesians.
- Luminoth: winged starfarers who settled Aether, now thriving under their last Sentinel, U-Mos.
- Ing: shapeshifting possessors born on Dark Aether after a Leviathan strike, ruled by the Emperor Ing; now extinct.
- Reptilicus: six-limbed reptilians of Bryyo, apparently descended from the Primals faction of the ancient Bryyonians; Phazon has stripped most of their reason.
- Elysians: robotic sentients built by the Chozo to study Phaaze, dwelling in SkyTown.
- Alimbics: mentally gifted colonizers of the Tetra Galaxy who sealed Gorea in the Oubliette and vanished.
- Lamorn: extinct psychic people of Viewros whose last priest, Chatoyant Vooloon, left recordings. The green energy they drew from Viewros's crystals to ripen a memory fruit made the wildlife aggressive and turned them into grievers.
- Hunter peoples: the Kriken, a vicious, feared race whose empire is expanded by exiles such as Trace; the Vhozon, proud, reclusive ice-adapted people of Vho, known only through Noxus; the Diamonts, an ancient silicon-based race of Mondreus, of whom Spire is thought to be the last; the Enoema, Kanden's kind, though he himself was created in a laboratory; the Phrygisians, ice-shaping natives of Phrygis, a moon of Bes III, known to Samus only through Rundas.
- Metroids, Chozo-engineered predators of SR388, and the X Parasites they were made to hunt. See Metroids and X Parasites.
- Unclassified: Sylux, a former Federation field captain whose species is never stated; Ghor (cyborg); Gandrayda (shape-shifter); Weavel (Space Pirate cyborg).

Samus herself is human by birth, Chozo by blood, and since the BSL incident carries Metroid DNA as well.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Human · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/K-2L · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Zebesian · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Luminoth · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ing · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Reptilicus</sub>


## Creatures


### Creatures: Fauna of Aether and Dark Aether

*Keys:* Aether wildlife, Aether creatures, Dark Aether wildlife, Dark Aether creatures, Alpha Splinter, Dark Splinter, Sandigger, Sandiggers, Grenchler, Grenchlers, Blogg, Bloggs, Alpha Blogg, Krocuss, Ingclaw, Ingclaws, Nightbarb, Nightbarbs, Lumite, Lumites, Mechlops, Kralee


Aether, the world the nomadic Luminoth settled and made their home, and its dark twin were catalogued by Samus during the Aether incident (Metroid Prime 2: Echoes).

Light Aether:
- Splinters (Temple Grounds, Great Temple): lightly armored insectoid pack hunters that hibernate in cocoons and wake in a frenzy at seismic shocks, releasing pheromones that rouse the rest. An Alpha Splinter leads each pack;
- Sandiggers (Agon Wastes): two-headed, two-brained tunnelers that ram and spit acidic digestive fluid; their hide resists weapons, leaving only the eyes exposed, and if one brain dies the other fights on. The Ing that stole Samus's Morph Ball Bombs possessed the pack's Alpha Sandigger.
- Kralees of Agon phase uncontrollably between dimensions, though the Green Kralee cannot.
- Lumites: photosynthetic moth-like insects, invisible and deadly in light, nearly harmless in shadow; among the few light creatures that survive on Dark Aether unpossessed.
- Bloggs (Torvus Bog): fast three-mawed aquatic hunters with damage-resistant hides and a sonic stun burst, weak only inside the open mouth; an Alpha Blogg leads, and Blogglings are the young.
- Grenchlers: gray bipedal reptiles with nine eyes a side that spring from hiding on land or water and fire electrical blasts; explosives crack their back armor to expose a nerve center.
- Krocuss: toxin-feeding mollusks; their shells shrug off most weapons, and they can be hurt only when the gills on their backs are exposed. Shriekers stun with sound and disrupt visors.
- Sanctuary Fortress holds Luminoth mechanoids. Mechlops are mechanical Triclops built after the Luminoth failed to train the real creatures for war. Rezbits transmit a crippling virus, Octopedes self-destruct, and Watchdrones can be driven off only by pure light.

Dark Aether:
- Darklings include light-world creatures possessed by Ing (Dark Splinters, Dark Grenchlers, Dark Bloggs, Dark War Wasps and others, along with possessed Space Pirate troopers) and small airborne natives such as Nightbarbs, black-and-violet three-winged fliers that travel in packs because Hunter Ing prey on them.
- Ingclaws are rooted Ing whose shells no weapon in Samus's arsenal breaks; they feed on atmospheric flotsam and vent toxic dark vapor, which the Dark Suit later let her ignore.
- Lightbringers, crystalline foragers the Luminoth bio-engineered for their war with the Ing, roam the ledges and bridges of the Dark Agon Wastes radiating a protective field of life-giving energy; only a matter-antimatter blast destroys them.
- Warrior Ing, Hunter Ing, the Emperor Ing and other Ing are recorded separately.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime_2:_Echoes_Logbook · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Luminoth · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Aether_Wildlife · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Dark_Aether_Wildlife · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Agon_Wastes_Wildlife · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Torvus_Bog_Wildlife</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of Bryyo

*Keys:* Bryyo wildlife, Bryyo creatures, Bryyonian wildlife, Reptilicus Hunter, Reptilicus Hunters, Alpha Hopper, Gragnol, Gragnols, Fargul Hatcher, Fargul Wasps, Warp Hound, Warp Hounds, Korba, Gel Puffer, Gel Puffers, Gel Ray, Gel Rays, Scorchbug, Shelbug, Bryyonian Shriekbat


Bryyo's surviving life is savage and Phazon-touched, catalogued by Samus during the Phazon crisis (Metroid Prime 3: Corruption) while she hunted the planet's Leviathan Seed.

- Reptilicus Hunters are Reptilicus corrupted by Phazon, stronger and tougher than the common kind. Phazon gave them a cloak that only the X-Ray Visor pierces; they fight with an energy whip up close, a thrown chakram at range, and claws, but lack the common Reptilicus's short-range teleport. Samus first fought them in Bryyo Cliffside's Grand Court. The Reptilicus themselves are recorded under Bryyo's history.
- Warp Hounds serve the Reptilicus as guards and hunting beasts. They live on Fuel Gel, teleport over short distances in a way that scrambles electronic targeting, and are vulnerable to cold.
- Hoppers are somewhat fragile insectoids that close in with small hops and pounce, commonly hunting in packs. The Alpha Hopper has a durable hide, attacks anything that strays near its nest, and in a pack hangs back while others strike.
- Gragnols are weakly armored young that attack in groups; the Gragnol Adult carries an energy shield and a hard exoskeleton with a vulnerable tail.
- Fargul Hatchers, found only in the Hall of the Golems, have skin that deflects most weapon fire and an organ cluster vulnerable only when exposed; their Fargul Wasp offspring swarm any attacker.
- Korba are resilient predators fixed for life to cavern ceilings above narrow tunnels. Symbiotic Snatchers, worm-like swarmers, seize prey, inflate and float it up to the Korba, which expels its leftovers to feed them.
- Gel Puffers float on Fuel Gel vapor behind thin shells. Nonaggressive Gel Rays are among the few creatures that live in the caustic Fuel Gel pools, feeding on the small organisms within. Gelbugs and Scorchbugs are gel-filled insects that explode when shot. Soft-bodied Shelbugs cling to the walls of the Thorn Jungle and extend or fire sharp spines.
- Bryyonian Shriekbats are bat-like dive-bombers. Nightbarbs here are aggressive chargers wrapped in an unstable energy field that overloads on contact and kills them. Geemers scavenge waste and spread disease behind shells only explosives crack.
- Phaazoids, floating Phazon life forms harmed only by Phazon-based attacks that split in two when struck down, appeared on Bryyo after Samus destroyed its Seed.

The Korakk Beast, Mogenar, the Bryyonians and the corrupted hunter Rundas are recorded separately.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime_3:_Corruption_Logbook · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Bryyonian_Wildlife · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bryyo · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Reptilicus · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Reptilicus_Hunter · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gragnol</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of Crateria and Brinstar

*Keys:* Zoomer, Zoomers, Geemer, Geemers, Skree, Skrees, Metaree, Metarees, Reo, Reos, Sidehopper, Sidehoppers, Side Hopper, Ripper II, Zebbo, Zebbos, Geega, Geegas, Beetom, Beetoms, Cacatac, Cacatacs, Zeela, Zeelas, Kago, Kagos, Fireflea, Firefleas, Samus Eater, Samus Eaters, Yapping Maw, Yapping Maws, Brinstar fauna, Crateria fauna, Zebes wildlife


Crateria is the storm-lashed surface of Zebes, pelted by meteors and acid rain; Brinstar is the humid cavern jungle beneath it, thick with carnivorous plants and home to more than sixty percent of the planet's life. Samus Aran grew up on Zebes among the Chozo. Per the manga, when Kreatz spotted a Skree hanging overhead she warned that the planet "has predators found nowhere else in the galaxy"; it then ripped through a Space Pirate travelling with them. She fought through both regions on her first Zebes mission (Metroid: Zero Mission) and again while hunting the stolen baby Metroid (Super Metroid).
- Zoomer and Geemer: small spiked crawlers that circle floors, walls and ceilings on fixed paths; per the manual they excrete a sticky liquid from their feet. Yellow Zoomers are the feeblest; red ones and the larger Geemer are far tougher. "Zoomer" began as a mistranslation of the Japanese name later rendered Geemer.
- Ripper: armor-shelled fliers (brainless, per the manual) drifting in straight lines. Beams and missiles bounce off; the Screw Attack or Speed Booster destroys one, and the Ice Beam freezes them into footholds. The jet-propelled Ripper II is faster.
- Skree: bat-like ambushers that hang from ceilings, drop on anything passing beneath, bore into the ground and burst into shrapnel. The armored Metaree is a tougher relative.
- Reo: clawed, fly-like divers swooping from the upper walls; per the manual, red ones outclass yellow.
- Waver: fliers whose two armored flaps close into a shield; they flutter and ram.
- Sidehopper: thick-legged pouncers that can even hop along ceilings. Three oversized ones in Tourian could be hurt only by Super Missiles, and the baby Metroid killed the last before Samus could.
- Zeb, Zebbo and Geega: insects that pour one after another from Brinstar's pipes and air holes to dive at intruders.
- Beetom: leapers that latch on and cling like miniature Metroids; bombs and missiles harm them, beams do not.
- Cacatac: walking cactus-plants firing thorn volleys.
- Zeela: eyestalked crawlers on fixed paths.
- Kago: insect nests that erupt in swarms only if disturbed.
- Fireflea: bioluminescent drifters, the only light in Brinstar's darkest rooms.
- Samus Eater and Yapping Maw: a flower whose petals clamp shut on prey, and a buried pincer-worm that lunges out to seize passers-by.
See also: Kraid, Spore Spawn, the Torizo, the Etecoons, the Dachora, Zebesians and Kihunters.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Brinstar · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Crateria · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Zoomer · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Geemer · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Ripper · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Skree</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of Elysia and the Pirate Homeworld

*Keys:* Elysia wildlife, Elysia creatures, SkyTown creatures, Pirate Homeworld creatures, Pirate Homeworld wildlife, Steambot, Steambots, Steamlord, Steamlords, Tinbot, Tinbots, Aeromine, Aeromines, Elysian Shriekbat, Elysian Shriekbats, Phaazoid, Phaazoids, Red Phaazoid, Puffer Mine, Puffer Mines, Phazon Harvester Drone


Elysia's wildlife is mostly machinery. Its inhabitants, the Elysians, are mechanoids the Chozo built to run SkyTown; after Dark Samus's Leviathan struck, Phazon corruption turned many of them and their drones hostile, as Samus found during the Phazon crisis (Metroid Prime 3: Corruption).

- Steamlords are Phazon-corrupted Elysians that command SkyTown's worker drones and repair them when they break down. Lightly armored, they cloak themselves and emit electromagnetic pulses that scramble visors. Samus met three: in the Steambot Barracks she could strike one only while it paused to repair a Steambot; on Skybridge Hera the bridge collapsed beneath the second; the X-Ray Visor exposed the third in Ballista Storage.
- Steambots are Chozo-built combat units that fight under a Steamlord, made of tough metal too heavy to throw and armed with kinetic-beam pistols, missile racks and tool-arms. A Steamlord can rebuild a fallen Steambot, but high heat disables one permanently.
- Tinbots, lightweight Elysians converted to security patrols, carry weak kinetic pistols; explosions or a Boost Ball send them sprawling and a charged Plasma Beam shot melts them.
- Elysian Shriekbats are Chozo machines built to mimic real Shriekbats: they hang beneath the floating facilities in small flocks and dive-bomb intruders, detonating on contact.
- Aeromines are Space Pirate drones deployed in hexagonal groups of eight (also on Norion, Bryyo and the G.F.S. Olympus) that attack one at a time and self-destruct at close range. Shield and beam share one power source, so a firing Aeromine drops its shield; the Pirates executed the first design team for the flaw.
- Phaazoids and the stronger Red Phaazoids, floating Phazon life forms immune to conventional weapons that split when destroyed, drifted through SkyTown after Samus destroyed Elysia's Seed.

The Pirate Homeworld, lashed by acid rain that eats through armor, is native ground for Puffers. The Space Pirates cybergenetically altered them into Puffer Mines, Phazon-gas-filled patrol animals that rupture on contact in a toxic cloud few attackers survive. The Phazon Harvester Drone, a Goliath-class heavy assault drone refitted with a Harvesting Beam Cannon and anti-aircraft missiles, has a hull no man-portable weapon penetrates; Samus stunned its sensor array, overloaded its anti-air system and called in her gunship's missiles to destroy it. Phaazoids appeared here too once the final Seed fell. The Phazon Metroids held in the Pirates' Xenoresearch laboratories, Omega Ridley, Gandrayda and the Pirate Commander are recorded separately.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Steambot · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Steamlord · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Tinbot · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Aeromine · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Elysian_Shriekbat · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Phaazoid</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of Maridia and the Wrecked Ship

*Keys:* Mochtroid, Mochtroids, Evir, Evirs, Owtch, Owtches, Puyo, Puyos, Powamp, Powamps, Zoa, Zoas, Tatori, Tatoris, Oum, Oums, Choot, Choots, Sciser, Scisers, Skultera, Skulteras, Covern, Coverns, Koben, Maridia fauna, Wrecked Ship creatures


Maridia is the flooded quarter of Zebes bordering Brinstar and Crateria: underwater tunnels, sandy caverns and quicksand, home to Draygon, Botwoon and a submerged Space Pirate research facility. Samus Aran reached it only after taking the Gravity Suit from the Wrecked Ship, breaking open a tunnel in the middle of Brinstar with a Power Bomb, and came away with the Space Jump, Plasma Beam and Spring Ball while hunting the stolen baby Metroid (Super Metroid). The Wrecked Ship is a crashed vessel on Crateria's surface, left powerless by Phantoon.
Maridia:
- Mochtroid: per the manual, "the Space Pirates' first attempt at Metroid clones"; frail things that drain energy but cannot latch on. They were Samus's first evidence that the Pirates were cloning Metroids.
- Evir: hard-shelled drifters that ride the sand currents, firing projectiles even while buried; a swarm attended Draygon and carried its corpse away.
- Owtch: spike-shelled crawlers that burrow into sand when they sense danger.
- Puyo: small green blobs that hop at intruders in short arcs.
- Powamp: placid gas-sacs that inflate when threatened and serve as anchors for the Grapple Beam.
- Zoa: shrimp-like hoverers that rise from the sand one after another in groups.
- Yard: clawed snails that climb any surface and withdraw into armored shells; only explosives harm them.
- Tatori: a turtle-like giant easily mistaken for a mossy rock, violently protective of its young.
- Oum: nautilus-like rollers with indestructible shells; only three were ever seen.
- Bull: hollow, gas-filled spore sacs that vent gas to ram intruders; they also drift through the Wrecked Ship.
- Choot: flat amphibians that leap straight up and glide down onto passers-by.
- Sciser and Skultera: red armored crustaceans that climb the underwater walls, and bony armored fish on fixed patrol routes.
Wrecked Ship:
- Covern (Koben): skull-clusters the manual calls "souls of shipwrecked creatures" that "wrap themselves around intruders"; they materialize while the power is out and vanish once it returns.
- Atomic: beings of pure energy sealed in glass chambers until the power returns, then drifting free to shock anything they touch.
- Work Robot: slow, cylindrical bipedal machines, inert until the ship wakes, then firing ring-beams at anything that moves; indestructible.
Kago nests, Cacatacs, Yapping Maws and Boyons round out the two regions. See also: Phantoon, Draygon, Botwoon.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Maridia · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Super_Metroid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Wrecked_Ship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Mochtroid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Evir · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Owtch</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of Norfair

*Keys:* Sova, Sovas, Gamet, Gamets, Geruta, Gerutas, Multiviola, Multiviolas, Squeept, Squeepts, Alcoon, Alcoons, Magdollite, Magdollites, Fune, Funes, Namihe, Namihes, Mella, Mellas, Memu, Memus, Puromi, Dessgeega, Dessgeegas, Desgeega, Norfair fauna, Norfair wildlife


Norfair is the volcanic underworld of Zebes: lakes of magma, superheated caverns and boiling streams of acid, with Ridley's lair in its depths and, by Samus Aran's second visit, a massive Space Pirate base. Samus crossed Norfair on both of her Zebes missions. On the second (Super Metroid) she found it heavily flooded with lava since her first visit and survivable only in the Varia Suit, and in Ridley's lair she found the baby Metroid's broken capsule.
- Nova (also recorded as Sova): crawlers covered, per the manual, in "spiky, fireproof wool," creeping along floors and ceilings, dripping flaming fluid; blue ones are weaker than yellow. Some were later taken to the BSL station and the Bottle Ship.
- Gamet: hard-shelled fliers that burst from air holes or magma in vertical stacks to dive at a target; per the manual, red Gamets have twice the strength of the others.
- Geruta: armored, hot-blooded fliers with huge forearms that swoop in wave-like glides to rake prey; red ones are the stronger kind.
- Multiviola and Viola: fiery orange orbs that careen through caverns rebounding off walls, and their blue larval form, bound to patches of rock.
- Holtz: armored hoverers of lower Norfair and Ridley's lair that hang still, then swoop on jets of ignited bio-propellant, even through lava.
- Squeept: crustacean-like leapers that spring from magma pools to grab prey; freezable into stepping stones.
- Dragon and Alcoon: seahorse-shaped fire-spitters that rise from lava and sink back, and a bipedal relative that leaps from beneath the ground spitting three fireballs (red in Norfair, green in Crateria).
- Magdollite: hulking lava-coated humanoids that lurk with only their faces above the surface, then fling globs of lava; only Super Missiles or Power Bombs kill them.
- Fune and Namihe: serpents living inside rock walls with only their heads showing (the Fune eyeless, hunting by hearing), spitting globules that burst into flame. A Fune can be destroyed or frozen; a Namihe can only be frozen briefly.
- Mella and Memu: tiny swarming fliers; the fiercer Memu infest Ridley's hideout and pass through walls.
- Puromi: indestructible fiery serpents of an acid pool.
- Dessgeega: larger, spine-studded relatives of the Sidehopper, aggressive on sight.
Zebbos rise from Norfair's acid, and Cacatacs, Kagos and Yapping Maws persist here. See also: Ridley, Crocomire, the Golden Torizo.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Norfair · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Super_Metroid · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Nova · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sova · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Gamet · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Squeept</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of Phaaze and the G.F.S. Valhalla

*Keys:* Phaaze wildlife, Phaaze creatures, Phaaze fauna, Phaz-Ing, Phaz-Ings, Phazon Grub, Phazon Grubs, Liquid Phazon, Phazon Hopper, Phazon Hoppers, Jelsac, Jelsacs, Phazon Shriekbat, Phazon Shriekbats, Phazon Nightbarb, Phazon Nightbarbs, Valhalla creatures, Valhalla wildlife


Phaaze is a sentient planet of living Phazon, source of every Leviathan, and everything that lives on it is built from or saturated with Phazon. Samus landed there at the end of the Phazon crisis (Metroid Prime 3: Corruption); its Phazon-laden atmosphere locked her suit permanently into Hypermode and drove her corruption steadily higher.

- Phaz-Ings are wall-crawling shape-shifters that rest as puddles of Phazon and rise into solid form only to hurl Phazon bursts. They cross any terrain and keep their distance. When solid they shimmer yellow, and a single Grapple Lasso pull tears them apart; Samus also vented her own excess Phazon into them with the Hyper Grapple, destroying them. They move in groups on Phaaze and infest the derelict G.F.S. Valhalla, where they arrived with the Space Pirate boarders and survive on the ship's Phazon.
- Phazon Grubs are immune to Phazon's mutagenic effects and carry it in their bodies; their bite infects victims with corruption, making them plague bearers. Drawn to bioenergy, they lunge at it. They are the larval form of Liquid Phazon, which spawns them in numbers.
- Liquid Phazon is a semisentient mobile puddle, the adult form of the Grub, that consumes Phazon in any form, releases toxins when threatened and reshapes itself to dodge attacks; contact can be deadly. It also occurs on Norion and aboard the Valhalla.
- Tangle Weed, the Tallon IV plant, grows Phazon-enhanced in Phaaze's Drop Shaft, where it damages as well as snares and evades. Phazon Hoppers and Hopping Metroids share the shaft, harrying anyone who tries to destroy the red Phazon crystals at its center. Samus shattered those crystals with the Hyper Ball, and every creature in the shaft died with them.
- Jelsacs and Phazon Metroids pour constantly through small tunnels into the Genesis Chamber, the nursery where Infant Leviathans grow until they rise to the surface, and they attacked Samus as she fought to destroy its Womb. Miniroids live there too, and the chamber's walls hold husks resembling Metroid Prime, of unknown origin.
- Phazon Shriekbats are Phazon-dependent, more aggressive than their kin, and die in a Phazon explosion when they dive. Phazon Nightbarbs are a separate Nightbarb species with Phazon-based attacks.

The G.F.S. Valhalla, the Federation ship the Pirates gutted for Aurora Unit 313, also carries Atomics, Hopping Metroids and Miniroids. Dark Samus, Aurora Unit 313 and the Metroid strains are recorded separately.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Phaaze · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Phaz-Ing · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Phazon_Grub · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Liquid_Phazon · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Tangle_Weed · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Genesis_Chamber</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of SR388

*Keys:* Hornoad, Hornoads, Tsumuri, Tsumuris, Moheek, Moheeks, Gravitt, Gravitts, Ramulken, Ramulkens, Yumbo, Yumbos, Septogg, Septoggs, Senjoo, Senjoos, Gullugg, Gulluggs, Yumee, Yumees, Gawron, Gawrons, Halzyn, Halzyns, Chute Leech, Chute Leeches, Octroll, Octrolls, Blob Thrower, Blob Throwers, Skorp, Skorps, Skreek, Skreeks, Wallfire, Wallfires, Autoad, Autoads, Autrack, Autracks, Autom, Automs, Gunzoo, Gunzoos, SR388 fauna, SR388 creatures


SR388, the Metroid homeworld, is a maze of caverns, acid pools and abandoned Chozo ruins whose machines still patrol. The Chozo created the Metroids there to combat the X parasites, which had killed everything on the planet like a plague. Samus Aran crossed SR388 on her Metroid extermination mission (Metroid II / Samus Returns). Years later, escorting a Biologic Space Laboratories research team on the surface, she shot a Hornoad and was infected by the X that emerged from it; the planet was destroyed at the end of the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion).
- Hornoad: frog-like hoppers with clawed feet that attack intruders but quickly lose interest if they move away. Per the manga, Old Bird described it to his fellow Chozo as a "silly creature that hops around" that turns hostile once an X mimics it. After Samus's extermination mission, a Hornoad tried to eat one of Proteus Ridley's severed arms and an X emerged and took it over.
- Tsumuri, Needler and Moheek: snail-, urchin- and anemone-like crawlers on walls and ceilings, harmless unless touched.
- Moto: armored, territorial chargers, soft only at the rear.
- Gravitt and Ramulken: a crab that hides in sand disguised as spikes and scuttles off when disturbed, and an armored one-eyed crustacean vulnerable only at its underbelly.
- Yumbo, Mumbo, Septogg and Senjoo: weak patrolling fliers; the Yumbo bears a spinning spiked appendage before its face, the docile Septogg can briefly carry Samus, and the Senjoo rams with spiked rings.
- Gullugg: big-eyed, bat-winged circlers.
- Yumee and Gawron: nest insects that erupt one after another.
- Drivel and Skreek: bat-like hunters of the deepest caverns that drop corrosive, explosive saliva, and lava-lurking ambushers that leap out spitting paralytic blobs.
- Halzyn: helium-shelled drifters, harmless until X infection taught them to snap shut and drop.
- Chute Leech and Octroll: flat, camouflaged leapers that spring up and glide down onto prey, and a cephalopod relative floating in acid pools.
- Blob Thrower: a huge rooted flower that spews spores.
- Glow Fly and Skorp: wall-clinging glowers that are the only natural light underground, and wall-burrowing arachnids with sawblade claws.
Chozo machines: Wallfire (bird-shaped wall sentries spitting fireballs), Autoad (hopping robots "out of control"), Autrack (laser guardians with extending necks), Autom (flamethrower units), Gunzoo (armored security drones) and Shirk (spiked circling drones). See also: Arachnus, the Diggernaut, Metroids, X Parasites.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/SR388 · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Hornoad · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Samus_Returns · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Tsumuri · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Needler</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of Tallon IV

*Keys:* Tallon IV wildlife, Tallon IV creatures, Tallon IV fauna, Sheegoth, Sheegoths, Baby Sheegoth, Magmoor, Triclops, Flickerbat, Flickerbats, Jelzap, Jelzaps, Blastcap, Blastcaps, Tangle Weed, War Wasp, War Wasps, Shriekbat, Shriekbats, Lumigek


Tallon IV's wildlife is known chiefly through the Logbook scans Samus made during the Tallon IV incident (Metroid Prime), when Phazon from the Leviathan impact was already warping the planet's ecosystem.

Tallon Overworld and Chozo Ruins:
- Beetles burrow underground and erupt underfoot; the armored Plated Beetle can be hurt only from behind. Zoomers and Geemers are small spined wall-crawlers.
- Blastcaps are toxic mushrooms that rattle a warning, then burst at the slightest touch into venomous fumes; their spores ride solar winds and have reached Alinos in the Alimbic Cluster.
- Tangle Weed and Venom Weed are barbed, dimly sentient plants: Tangle Weed can only slow larger creatures, while Venom Weed lures prey and digests what it kills. Bloodflowers spew toxic spores; Seedlings fire dorsal spines.
- Jelzaps top the aquatic food chain: two armored halves bound only by electrical impulses, which lure prey with an exposed core and snap shut. Tallon Crabs swarm, Aqua Reapers are blind underwater tentacles, and Reaper Vines are scythe-tipped tentacles rooted in rock.
- Stone Toads sit motionless in stone armor and swallow prey whole.
- War Wasps dive-bomb with venomous stingers and rarely stray from their hives; Ram War Wasps share a hive mind and Barbed War Wasps launch their stingers.
- Eyons are immobile eyes that fire energy beams. Shriekbats roost on ceilings at an internal temperature near 121 degrees Celsius and explode when they dive. Pulse Bombus and Scatter Bombus are energy beings harmed only by electrical attacks.

Magmoor Caverns: the Magmoor is a fire-breathing lava serpent that tracks prey by smell and suffers in cold. Puffers drift gas-filled and rupture into lethal meta-viprium gas. Triclops are hard-shelled hunter-gatherers whose three-part mandibles mistake Samus's Morph Ball for rock or food and carry it off to be dumped; only a Morph Ball Bomb fed to one kills it. Puddle Spores are lava mollusks that fire energy globules; Grizbies carry superheated carapaces.

Phendrana Drifts: the Sheegoth is the supreme predator of the Drifts. Crystals on its back absorb energy and fire it back, its frigid breath freezes prey, and it gasps open-mouthed after breathing; only the mouth and soft underbelly are open to harm. Baby Sheegoths turn to shield their weak backs. Samus first met the species in the Chapel of the Elders, where an adult and a group of young guarded the Wave Beam. Flickerbats are optically camouflaged scavengers that flicker in and out of sight and can be tracked reliably only with the X-Ray Visor. Gliders are docile fliers with magnetic undersides; Samus latched onto them with the Grapple Beam, the same trick behind the sport of Glider riding. Ice Shriekbats, Ice Beetles, Crystallites and Ice Parasites fill the cold niches.

Impact Crater: Lumigeks, Phazon-charged reptiles that move in swarms.

Chozo Ghosts, Tallon Metroids, Flaahgra, Thardus and Meta Ridley are recorded separately.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime_Logbook · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Tallon_IV_Wildlife · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Impact_Crater_Wildlife · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Tallon_IV · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sheegoth · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Magmoor</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of the Alimbic Cluster

*Keys:* Alimbic Cluster creatures, Alimbic Cluster wildlife, Alimbic wildlife, Psycho Bit, Psycho Bits, Greater Ithrak, Lesser Ithrak, Ithraks, Petrasyl, Petrasyls, Quadtroid, Quadtroids, Voldrum, Voldrums, Arctic Spawn, Fire Spawn, Crash Pillar, Crash Pillars, Trocra


The Alimbic Cluster's life forms were logged by Samus during the Alimbic Cluster incident (Metroid Prime Hunters), on worlds the vanished Alimbics had left guarded by sentry machines alongside native and immigrant animals.

Celestial Archives:
- Lesser Ithraks are nocturnal ceiling-clingers, fierce when disturbed; the rare Greater Ithrak looks identical, but its impenetrable frontal hide makes head-on attack useless, leaving only its rear open.
- Petrasyls are green, tentacled drifters on simple paths; harmless at a distance, they secrete a corrosive toxin that burns through almost any material on contact.
