- Quadtroids, engineered from leech and reptile DNA, cling to almost any surface, pounce and latch on to drain biological energy; only a Morph Ball Bomb reliably breaks their grip. They also haunt the Vesper Defense Outpost.
- Psycho Bits are levitating spherical sentries with 360-degree sensors and short-range teleportation that emerge in swarms from blue replicator devices. The v1.0 fires energy blasts; v2.0 drains energy with high-voltage electromagnetic pulses; v3.0 (Alinos, Vesper Defense Outpost) spits superheated magma; v4.0 (Vesper Defense Outpost, Arcterra) freezes prey with supercooled plasma.
- Voldrums are fast rolling robots with twin guns. Ice Voldrums fire freezing shots, but their cryocoolers fail under high heat; Magma Voldrums run hot and fall to subzero weapons; the Electro Voldrum's thinner hull breaks under rapid fire.
- Guardians are automatons built to guard the Alimbic Order and still serving in its absence, travelling in packs and firing from three small fusion generators in their heads. Crash Pillars are dormant cyborg sentries that wake at intruders and levitate to crush them.

Alinos: Fire Spawn lurk in lava pits, breathing superheated lava and hurling fireballs at anything entering their territory. Red Barbed War Wasps fire igneous projectiles. War Wasps defend hives whose royal jelly is prized enough to be harvested at great cost. Zoomers breed fast and spread disease; on Zebes they helped spread a flesh-eating bacterium that wiped out many native species. Blastcaps and Shriekbats, both Tallon IV natives, have spread here.

Arcterra: Arctic Spawn are territorial cold-dwellers that hurl immobilizing ice shards; Blue Barbed War Wasps, frozen-environment mutants, fire cold projectiles.

Oubliette: Trocra are crystallized bacterial colonies so unstable they detonate on touching a solid surface.

Alimbic Turrets, Cretaphids and Slenches are Alimbic defense machinery guarding the Octoliths; Gorea and the rival hunters Kanden, Spire, Noxus, Trace, Weavel and Sylux are recorded separately.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime_Hunters_Logbook · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Alimbic_Cluster_Life_Forms · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Alinos_Wildlife · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Arcterra_Wildlife · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Celestial_Archives_Life_Forms · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Vesper_Defense_Outpost_Life_Forms</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of the Bermuda System

*Keys:* Bermuda System, Excelcion, Bion, Talvania, Goliath Beetle, Goliath Beetles  
*Secondary (AND ANY):* creature, creatures, wildlife, fauna, beetle, beetles, animals, monsters


The Bermuda System holds three planets, Excelcion, Bion and Talvania, each of which housed a Space Pirate cloaking generator shielding the battleship Doomseye during the Bermuda System incident (Metroid Prime: Federation Force). Its wildlife is thinly recorded. The Federation Force's missions there ranged from icy regions to desert, and apart from Space Pirates and Metroids the only creature named in published accounts is the Goliath Beetle, which the Force fought in addition to the Pirates. Nothing more of its biology is recorded.

Metroids, the jellyfish-like energy parasites, were in Space Pirate hands in the system. A Metroid egg the Force stole from the Pirates ended up in a Galactic Federation facility, where a figure strongly resembling Sylux broke in and projected a beam onto it, hatching it rapidly into a larva.

On Bion the Force learned that the Pirates had acquired an ancient technology that swelled them to gargantuan size. Samus herself was enlarged to gigantic size and forced to fight the Force; their victory restored her normal size, and she later rescued them as the Federation Fleet destroyed the Doomseye.

No other native animals of Bermuda are recorded; the Doomseye and the Federation Force are covered separately.


<sub>Sources: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime:_Federation_Force · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Prime:_Federation_Force · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Metroid_Prime:_Federation_Force</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of the Bottle Ship

*Keys:* Kyratian, Kyratians, Himella, Himellas, Ghalmanian, Ghalmanians, Griptian, Griptians, Mighty Griptian, Dragotix, Groganch, Fumbleye, Fumbleyes, Joulion, Joulions, Volfon, Volfons, Gigafraug, Gigafraugs, Rhedogian, Rhedogians, Desbrachian, Desbrachians, Brug, Brugs, Brug Mass, Emperor Brug, Cyclaw, Cyclaws, Bottle Ship creatures, Bottle Ship fauna


The Bottle Ship was a Galactic Federation vessel built, against Federation protocol, to cultivate bioweapons across its sectors: the jungle Sector 1 (Biosphere), the frigid Sector 2 (Cryosphere), the volcanic Sector 3 (Pyrosphere), the Main Sector and the Bioweapon Research Center. Many of its specimens were clones of Zebes species, so when Samus Aran investigated alongside Adam Malkovich's 07th Platoon (Metroid: Other M) she met species she knew from that destroyed planet. A Cryosphere Gigafraug corpse bearing signs of Metroid predation warned her that Metroids were loose aboard.
Zebes clones:
- Reo, Geemer and Skree: Reos burst from biomass nests and grip Samus; cloned Skrees do not self-destruct and dive repeatedly. The Cyclaw is a seemingly mechanically enhanced Skree.
- Zero: Kihunter larvae that leap at intruders and, left undisturbed, pupate into cocoons from which adult Kihunters emerge.
- Mella and Himella: flame-spitting swarmers and their ice-shard-firing Cryosphere kin.
- Waver, Sidehopper, Dessgeega, Nova, Magdollite, Skultera, Rinka, Fune and Namihe also live aboard.
Natives and designs:
- Ghalmanian: chameleon-like Biosphere creatures that turn invisible, then headbutt or spit acid.
- Griptian and Mighty Griptian: armadillo-like beasts that roll into balls to ram and burrow.
- Dragotix: large creatures, docile unless something enters their zone of perception.
- Groganch: six-legged, tree-like giants camouflaged by bark-like skin, firing gas-filled globes from an orange abdominal bulb.
- Kyratian: amphibian-like Cryosphere humanoids with electrical coils on their backs that hurl electric orbs.
- Fumbleye, Joulion, Volfon and Gigafraug, also of the Cryosphere: a one-eyed ceiling polyp that grabs victims and flings them into water; hovering water-flea creatures that shock and explode; horned beasts reported to hunt in packs of three; and a massive armored brute.
- Rhedogian: one-eyed flying behemoths that ram, fire Seeker Missiles and charge an eye laser that can kill outright. One ambushed Samus on a Cryosphere elevator after she cut down seven Super Zebesians, attacked Anthony Higgs above the Pyrosphere's lava until she rescued him, and died in the Biosphere, where she took its Seeker Missiles.
- Desbrachian: dormant ovoid creatures disguised as the alarms above doors; a Power Bomb wakes them, and MB woke them telepathically to annihilate Federation soldiers. They move at high speed, grab and slam.
- Brug: tiny swarmers that gather around an Emperor Brug into a single Brug Mass.
See also: Vorash, Goyagma, King Kihunter, Nightmare, the Ridley clone, the Queen Metroid, Phantoon, MB.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid:_Other_M · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Bottle_Ship · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Sector_2_(Cryosphere) · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Bottle_Ship_Life_Forms · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Sector_1_(Biosphere)_Life_Forms · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Reo</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of the BSL station

*Keys:* Zero-X, Zoro-X, Zombie Researcher, Zombie Researchers, Yameba, Yamebas, Genesis-X, Geruboss, Geruda, Gelon, Gelons, Zozoro, Memu-X, Sciser-X, Hornoad-X, Halzyn-X, Gold Sciser, Gold Scisers, BSL creatures, BSL fauna, BSL specimens


The Biologic Space Laboratories research station (BSL) orbited SR388 and kept specimens from that planet, from Zebes and from farther afield in sealed habitats: Sector 1 (SRX, an SR388 analogue), Sector 2 (TRO, tropical), Sector 3 (PYR, volcanic), Sector 4 (AQA, aquatic), Sector 5 (ARC, arctic), Sector 6 (NOC, nocturnal) and the Main Deck. During the BSL incident (Metroid Fusion) the X parasites killed and mimicked nearly all of them, so the creatures Samus Aran met were X copies, often larger, more aggressive or newly armed, each releasing an X she could absorb through her Fusion Suit. Adam, her AI commanding officer, warned that the X were evolving and that some now resisted her weapons. Samus knew many of the originals from Zebes and SR388.
Main Deck and SRX:
- Hornoad: mimicked ones spit damaging fluid, while the few uninfected ones never attack.
- Zombie Researcher: station crew taken by the X, per the manual "probably the result of X parasite absorbing a slime creature's DNA"; shambling, weak, and regenerated whenever an X re-enters the body. Adam judged that the X infected the humans for knowledge and that the others were merely food.
- Moto: territorial armored chargers, soft only at the rear.
- Halzyn: helium-shelled drifters that snap shut and drop on whatever is beneath.
- Yameba: floating amoeba-like X masses that settle on prey and drain it, merging into larger blobs.
- Genesis: large crawlers in dark ceiling spaces, drooling caustic saliva.
- Geruboss and Geruda: X-enhanced Gerutas; the Geruboss has metallic claws and shrugs off beams, the Geruda glides upright.
TRO: Zero-X (also rendered Zoro), the Kihunter larvae, red and far tougher blue; Reo, Ripper, Dessgeega, spiked red Puyo, Skultera, and Kago nests swollen into damaging columns.
PYR: Sova leaving trails of flaming droppings, Sidehopper, Squeept, and Fune and Namihe striking from holes in the walls.
AQA: Skultera in large and golden varieties, Evir with orbiting projectiles, Owtch, nearly indestructible Powamp, Yard, and Sciser with thickened spiked shells and plasma-firing claws, clustering into Gold Scisers.
ARC: Ripper, Waver, Kago and the Chute, adapted by the X to the cold and armed with acidic vomit.
NOC: Memu, one-eyed swarmers, and Zozoro, large glowing cave worms clinging to ceilings.
Gelon: inert X masses blocking passages until cleared with missiles or Power Bombs.
See also: Zebesians, Kihunters, Blue X, Arachnus, Zazabi, Serris, Yakuza, Nettori, Nightmare, Neo-Ridley, SA-X, Adam Malkovich.


<sub>Sources: https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Metroid_Fusion · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:B.S.L_Research_Station_Life_Forms · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Sector_1_(SRX)_Life_Forms · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Sector_2_(TRO)_Life_Forms · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Sector_3_(PYR)_Life_Forms · https://www.metroidwiki.org/wiki/Category:Sector_4_(AQA)_Life_Forms</sub>


### Creatures: Fauna of Viewros

*Keys:* Viewros wildlife, Viewros creatures, Viewros fauna, Phenoros, Varmis, Keratos, Swim Snatcher, Swim Snatchers, Denzard, Denzards, Shren, Shrens, Aurengh Crane, Polaleisoarala, Dartwing, Dartwings, Crystal Crawler, Crystal Crawlers, Ghaspore, Sickleflies


Viewros is the Lamorn world to which a damaged Lamorn artifact on Tanamaar teleported Samus and everything around her (Metroid Prime 4: Beyond). Its wildlife turned aggressive when the Lamorn tried to nurture the planet with Green Energy, the same energy that transformed the Lamorn into Grievers, who are recorded separately. Several great beasts were further warped by fusion with Metroids.

Fury Green (jungle): Aurengh Cranes are fruit-eating birds that fly vast distances in flocks; Sickleflies are scissor-jawed dragonflies that swarm over decay; Glister Beetles signal with light, which betrays them to predators. Homing Spores drift after anything nearby and burst; Mireworms lure insects with a plant scent and swallow them whole; Ghaspores are hard-capped mushrooms vulnerable only while ejecting spores; Glottagroppers hide underground, shoot needles and leap; Bristleworts are slow six-legged carnivorous plants that fire thorns; Shrens build hives and spray acidic venom at intruders without pursuing. Carvex, a self-aware carnivorous plant that once spoke telepathically with Lamorn priests until Metroid fusion broke its thoughts, is recorded separately.

Volt Forge: Denzards, one-eyed arthropods whose organs run on electrical charge absorbed from the storm-darkened surroundings; their eye, teeth and tail glow.

Sol Valley (desert): Polaleisoarala are featherless birds powered by sunlight through a red membrane; Web Crawlers spin strong but flammable nests; Sand Shrens charge with needle jaws. The Varmis is a huge sand-swimming crustacean whose scaled back stops any firearm; Green Energy showers have made it grow steadily, and only its unscaled tail tip is vulnerable.

Ice Belt: Snow Wolves hunt in packs of several dozen, surround prey and feed in rank order behind the alpha, but their high metabolism leaves them vulnerable to heat. Snow Roaches draw kin with pheromones and glowing shells; Blue Krauks fly in groups behind an antenna-signalling leader; Dartwings, cold-adapted bats with poor sight, glide down at sounds. The Keratos, an armadillo-like giant twice Samus's height with an iceberg-like shell, spits icy boulders; once intelligent enough to convey its will to Lamorn priests, it was permanently altered in mind and body by Metroid fusion, which exposed its abdominal core.

Flare Pool (volcanic): Vaernans melt rock with digestive fluid; Heatstraps feed on seeping Green Energy; Blow Hoppers float on gas, swallowing and expelling lava; Leaping Spitters scatter lava bombs, while Swim Snatchers, the same species, roam the lake gathering energy-rich material with their feelers; Pyremites launch themselves and self-destruct; the Behemoth is an armored, fire-wielding arthropod vulnerable only through its four eyes. The Phenoros, a mineral giant over 150 meters long, swims the Magdrahda lava lake swallowing lava to extract Green Energy; Metroid fusion swelled the membrane joining its segments.

Great Mines: Crystal Crawlers eat Deborah crystals with six fangs, and Samus anchored Psychic Energy swings on the crystals of their shells. Winged Lizards keep to the shade and seldom attack unprovoked; Cave Shrens spit poison; Snare Weeds paralyze with electricity and burn readily; Pop Crawlers hurl glowing thorns.

The Lamorn-built Psy-bots, later turned against the Grievers, and the Volt Forge factory's Flight Drones and Mecha Rats are machines, not fauna.

### Creatures: Fauna of ZDR

*Keys:* Klaida, Klaidas, Muzby, Muzbys, Vulkran, Vulkrans, Autclast, Autclasts, Obsydomithon, Obsydomithons, Autsharp, Autsharps, Autsniper, Autsnipers, Armadigger, Armadiggers, Batalloon, Batalloons, Dizzean, Dizzeans, Slaaga, Slaagas, Hecathon, Hecathons, Yampa, Yampas, Quetzoa, Quetzoas, Sabotoru, Sabotorus, ZDR fauna, ZDR creatures


ZDR is the Chozo-held world where Samus Aran was stranded after Raven Beak stripped her abilities, hunted by the rogue E.M.M.I. (Metroid Dread). Its wildlife is native and often ancient; its research sites are patrolled by robots believed to use advanced Chozo technology. Samus met most of it with her Arm Cannon and the Melee Counter. After the X escaped quarantine in Elun they overran the whole planet, and creatures everywhere became X hosts, releasing an X when killed.
Artaria (the cold, cavernous depths where Samus awoke):
- Klaida: four-legged, unarmored natives that never attack but hurt and repel anything touching them.
- Ply: small fliers with an eyeball-like head that charge with a spiked tail, sometimes in groups.
- Muzby: large, faceless two-legged brutes with heavy arms and tough hide. An X-infected, mutated Muzby became Golzuna.
Cataris (where the planet's energy-generating magma is circulated):
- Vulkran: small creatures that cling to any surface, store magma and spit several shots when threatened.
- Autclast: robots whose side cylinders slam down, sending fire along the ground.
- Obsydomithon: rock-like creatures dormant as ore near magma until roused, then firing a heat blast that passes through terrain.
Dairon (a biological research site, partly dark from power failures):
- Autsharp and Autsniper: robots that launch from docks behind a spinning energy blade, and small treaded robots that climb walls and ceilings to fire a precise laser.
- Armadigger: violent four-legged natives that charge, wheel and charge again.
Burenia (a marine research site, its lower half underwater):
- Batalloon: deceptively durable floaters that drift toward whoever hits them.
- Dizzean: small jellyfish, weak alone but gathering in large groups.
- Slaaga: creatures that store acid in a yellow hump and fire it with precision.
Ghavoran (a sunlit underground forest near the surface):
- Hecathon: large creatures present on ZDR since ancient times; they usually ignore Samus but unleash a disintegrating light when struck.
- Yampa: spider-like stalkers that lunge with a strong bite but cannot reach a ledge above or below their own.
- Quetzoa: mid-sized insect-like fliers that home in and charge at high speed. A mutated Quetzoa became Escue.
Ferenia and Hanubia: Sabotoru, slug-like crawlers that rush to the nearest door and block it by spreading their bodies and extending spikes.
See also: Chozo Soldiers, Robot Chozo Soldiers, E.M.M.I., Corpius, Drogyga, Escue, Golzuna, Experiment Z-57, Mawkin.
